An upward-growing contiguous segment begins at physical byte address 1000 and currently has byte-length limit 200. Valid offsets are nonnegative and strictly smaller than that limit. The program needs 80 additional bytes, and the allocator has permission to assign the 80 physical bytes immediately following the segment. No other part of the segment moves during this successful extension.

| Physical byte range | State before extension |
|---|---|
| 1000 through 1199 | current segment |
| 1200 through 1279 | available for the extension |
| beginning at 1280 | a different live allocation |

Ignore descriptor storage and alignment overhead. Compare changing the length with changing the starting address, and find the final permitted byte after the extension. Also consider a counterfactual in which the immediately following region is already occupied: merely changing a limit must not turn someone else's live bytes into this program's storage. The method is pure contiguous segmentation rather than allocation of separate page frames. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Increasing the limit to 280 can accommodate the requested growth.
[x] The enlarged segment's final valid physical byte address is 1279.
[ ] The base must change to 1080 solely because the segment grows by 80 bytes.
[x] If the following bytes were occupied, merely increasing the limit could improperly expose another allocation.
[x] Growing in place works here only because the following bytes happen to be free.
---
「長度 280」對：200＋80。「最後地址 1279」對：最大位移 279，1000＋279＝1279。「base 加 80」錯：擴大長度不等於搬起點。「別人占用時不能硬擴」對：需要找可用空間、可能搬整段，或回報無法擴充。「靠後面剛好空著」對：連續分段不保證每次都能原地擴充。

@q me5-15 | os-memory#5 | 3 | 比較
In 32-bit x86 protected mode with paging enabled, a segment base is added to the offset to form a linear address before paging translates it. Let the segment base be 0x2000 and the offset 0x123. Use 4096-byte pages, and map linear page 2 to physical frame 9. Assume all checks pass. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The linear address is 0x2123.
[x] Segmentation precedes paging in this translation.
[x] The physical address differs from the linear address here, because paging maps page 2 to frame 9.
[x] The final physical address is 0x9123.
[x] Paging is applied to the linear address, not to the raw offset.
---
「線性 0x2123」對：0x2000＋0x123。「先分段再分頁」對：這是此模式的轉換次序。「實體不同於線性」對：分頁可改變對應。「實體 0x9123」對：4096＝0x1000，線性頁號 2、頁內位移 0x123；框 9 起點是 0x9000，加上位移為 0x9123。「分頁作用在線性位址」對：段基底先加進去，才交給分頁。五句都對；這裡明確限定 32 位元保護模式，不外推其他模式。

@q me5-16 | os-memory#5 | 2 | 證明
說明為什麼分頁沒有外部碎片、分段卻有。
---
分頁把實體記憶體切成固定大小的 frame，任何空閒 frame 都能給任何一頁用，不需要連續，所以不會有「總量夠但湊不出來」的情形；最多只是最後一頁用不滿（內部碎片）。分段的段大小不一、每段要一塊連續空間，配置和釋放後留下大小不一的空洞，就會有外部碎片。

@q vm1-07 | os-vm#1 | 2 | 計算
記憶體存取 100 ns、處理一次 page fault 10 ms，缺頁率 0.1%。有效存取時間約是不缺頁時的幾倍？
== 101
== 100
---
$0.999\times100+0.001\times10^7\approx10100$ ns，約 101 倍。千分之一的缺頁率就慢一百倍，所以缺頁率必須極低。

@q vm1-08 | os-vm#1 | 2 | 追蹤
處理 page fault 的步驟，正確順序？①找一個空的 frame（必要時換出一頁）②trap 進核心，確認位址合法 ③從磁碟讀入該頁 ④更新頁表、valid 設 1 ⑤重新執行那條指令
== 2 1 3 4 5
== 2,1,3,4,5
---
先確定是合法存取（不合法就終止行程）；讀磁碟期間 CPU 可以先給別的行程。最後一定要重做那條指令，因為它當初沒做完。

@q vm1-09 | os-vm#1 | 3 | 陷阱
一條指令要把 256 bytes 從 A 搬到 B（兩區可能重疊），搬到一半 B 的下一頁不在記憶體。處理完缺頁後「重新執行整條指令」會有什麼問題？
[x] 重疊時來源已經被前半段覆蓋，重做會搬錯；硬體要先確認所有頁都在，或記住進度
[ ] 沒有問題
[ ] 會無限缺頁
[ ] 會搬兩次但結果一樣
---
需求分頁要求每條指令都能「可重新開始」，有副作用的長指令要特別設計。

@q vm1-10 | os-vm#1 | 1 | 計算
page fault 時，被選中換出的頁被改過（修改位元是 1）。這次缺頁要做幾次磁碟 I/O？
== 2
---
先把髒頁寫回、再把要的頁讀進來。乾淨的頁直接丟掉，只要 1 次。

@q vm1-11 | os-vm#1 | 2 | 情境
A 200 MB photo editor has many rarely used features, and a typical run touches only 20 MB of its code and data; pages are 4 KiB. On a laptop, the editor is run with demand paging, so a page is read from disk only when it is first touched. The developers compare this with an older loader that reads all 200 MB into memory at start-up, and they measure start-up time, memory use, and the speed of the program's computations once it is running. Which of the following statements about running it with demand paging are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Only the 20 MB actually touched is loaded.
[x] Start-up is faster and less memory is used, so more programs can run at the same time.
[x] The first touch of each page costs one page fault.
[ ] Loading all 200 MB at start-up would be faster overall, since the disk is read only once.
[ ] The program's computations run faster once loaded, since its pages are smaller.
---
「只載入用到的 20 MB」：「啟動快、佔記憶體少」，可同時跑更多程式；代價是「每頁第一次碰到時缺頁一次」。全部載入要多讀 180 MB 用不到的東西；頁大小沒變，載入後的計算速度也一樣。

@q vm1-12 | os-vm#1 | 2 | 觀念
A process accesses a virtual address, and the page-table entry for its page is marked invalid, so the hardware traps to the operating system. The operating system keeps, for each process, a separate record of which parts of the address space are legal, such as code, data, heap, and stack, and of where each legal page currently lives. For example, in one process the legal addresses run from 0x400000 to 0x7fffff, and a heap page at 0x500000 has been written out to disk to free memory. The process now accesses 0x500010 in one case and 0x900000 in another, and a student must describe what the operating system does in each case, and what information it needs to tell the two cases apart, beyond the invalid bit itself. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The address may be illegal for this process, and then the process is terminated.
[x] The address may be legal with the page still on disk, and then the page is loaded.
[ ] The operating system only needs to tell reads from writes to decide what to do.
[ ] An invalid entry always means that the address is illegal, so the process is terminated.
[ ] An invalid entry always means that the page is on disk, so it is simply loaded.
---
valid 位元是 0 有兩種可能：「位址不合法就終止」，「合法但頁在磁碟上就載入」，作業系統查行程自己的合法區段記錄來分辨。不能一律終止或一律載入；讀或寫不是這裡要分的。

@q vm1-13 | os-vm#1 | 2 | 找錯
A student watching a performance tool sees that a word processor caused 1200 page faults in its first 10 seconds and then about 2 per minute, yet the program kept running normally. The student says: "A page fault means the program has a bug, and the operating system terminates it." A classmate explains that the system uses demand paging, so pages are brought in only when they are first touched, and asks the student to reconsider what a page fault is, where it can happen, and what happens after one. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] Under demand paging a page fault is normal, and execution continues after it is handled.
[ ] The student is right: the operating system terminates the process at every page fault.
[ ] A page fault makes the whole computer restart.
[ ] Page faults happen only in kernel code, never in user programs.
[ ] Every page fault is caused by an access to an illegal address.
---
需求分頁下「page fault 是正常的」，頁還沒載入而已，處理完就繼續。只有存取不合法的位址才終止（segmentation fault），不是每次缺頁都這樣；不會重開機；使用者程式最常觸發缺頁。

@q vm1-14 | os-vm#1 | 2 | 觀念
Demand paging works well for most programs, even though a page fault costs about $10^4$ times as much as a memory access. A student measures two programs with 4 KiB pages: a loop that sums an array of $10^6$ integers in order, which takes one fault per 1024 accesses, and a program that reads $10^6$ random positions of a 4 GB array, which faults on almost every access once the array no longer fits in memory, and the student compares their running times. Which of the following statements explain why demand paging performs acceptably in practice? Select all that apply.
[x] During any period of time, a program tends to use only a few pages.
[x] After a fault brings a page in, many later accesses to it hit.
[x] Random jumps over a huge array break this behavior and make demand paging very slow.
[ ] Disks are fast enough that each page fault costs about as much as a memory access.
[ ] Programs announce their memory accesses in advance, so pages are loaded just in time.
---
程式「在一段時間內只集中用少數頁」，「缺頁一次後很多存取都命中」；在大陣列上「亂跳」就沒有這種性質，需求分頁會非常慢。磁碟缺頁要毫秒級，比記憶體慢幾萬倍；程式也不會預先通知。

@q vm1-15 | os-vm#1 | 3 | 計算
記憶體 100 ns，缺頁率 0.01%。換頁裝置是固態硬碟（處理缺頁 0.1 ms）和傳統硬碟（8 ms），有效存取時間各約多少 ns？
== 110,900
?? 固態硬碟,傳統硬碟
---
固態：$100+10^{-4}\times10^5=110$；傳統：$100+10^{-4}\times8\times10^6=900$（忽略 $(1-p)$ 的微小差別）。

@q vm1-16 | os-vm#1 | 2 | 情境
程式 `malloc` 了 1 GB 但還沒寫入，作業系統常常不馬上給實體記憶體。第一次寫某頁時才配一個全 0 的 frame。這叫？
[x] 按需配零頁（zero-fill on demand）：也是一種需求分頁
[ ] 預先分頁
[ ] 換出
[ ] 記憶體洩漏
---
所以 `malloc` 很大的空間可能很快，真正寫的時候才逐頁缺頁。

@q vm1-17 | os-vm#1 | 3 | 比較
An operating system can start a program with pure demand paging, loading no page in advance, or it can load in advance a few pages around the program's entry point, guessing that they will be needed soon. A test program touches 40 distinct pages during its first 100 ms; each page fault costs about 8 ms, while reading a page in advance, as part of one larger disk read at start-up, costs about 1 ms. In one experiment, the advance loading guesses 30 pages, of which 24 are actually used, and in another it guesses 100 pages, of which only 25 are used. The developers want to understand the trade-off before choosing a default policy for all programs, and a manager argues that loading pages early can never hurt, because the disk is idle at start-up anyway. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Pure demand paging takes many faults in a row right at the start.
[x] Loading pages in advance reduces the faults at start-up.
[x] Loading pages that are never used is wasted work.
[x] Which is better depends on how accurately the needed pages are predicted.
[ ] Loading pages in advance is always better, since a page loaded early costs nothing.
---
「純需求分頁一開始連續缺頁」；「先載入能減少起步的缺頁」，但「載了用不到的是浪費」，所以「好壞看預測準不準」。提早載入也要花磁碟時間和記憶體，不是免費的。

@q vm1-18 | os-vm#1 | 2 | 計算
有 40% 的缺頁要把髒頁寫回。讀或寫一頁各 6 ms，忽略其他時間。平均處理一次缺頁要幾 ms？
== 8.4
---
$0.6\times6+0.4\times12=8.4$。dirty 位元讓乾淨的頁不用寫回，省下很多時間。

@q vm1-19 | os-vm#1 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼處理完 page fault 後要「重新執行」那條指令，而不是從下一條繼續。
---
缺頁發生在指令取用資料（或取指令本身）時，這條指令還沒完成它的工作（例如還沒把值載入暫存器）。從下一條繼續的話，這條指令的效果就消失了，程式結果錯誤。所以硬體要保存足夠的狀態，讓作業系統載入頁後把 PC 設回這條指令，從頭再做一次。

@q vm2-09 | os-vm#2 | 2 | 計算
參考串 4 7 0 7 1 0 1 2 1 2 7 1 2，3 個 frame。FIFO、LRU、OPT 各缺頁幾次？
== 6,6,5
?? FIFO,LRU,OPT
---
程式模擬三種。前三個都是強制缺頁（4、7、0）；之後 1、2、7 的處理不同，OPT 少一次。

@q vm2-10 | os-vm#2 | 2 | 計算
同一串（4 7 0 7 1 0 1 2 1 2 7 1 2）改成 4 個 frame，三種各缺頁幾次？
== 5,5,5
?? FIFO,LRU,OPT
---
不同的頁只有 5 個（4、7、0、1、2），4 個 frame 時只會多一次替換。frame 多到一定程度，演算法差別就消失。

@q vm2-11 | os-vm#2 | 2 | 追蹤
Clock：4 個 frame 的參考位元依序是 1、0、1、1，指針在第 0 個。要換頁時選中哪一個 frame？
== 1
---
第 0 個參考位元 1 → 清成 0、往下；第 1 個是 0 → 選它。

@q vm2-12 | os-vm#2 | 3 | 追蹤
Clock：4 個 frame 的參考位元都是 1，指針在第 2 個。要換頁時選中哪一個？
== 2
---
繞一圈把全部清成 0，回到第 2 個時它已經是 0，選它。全部都是 1 時 Clock 退化成 FIFO。

@q vm2-13 | os-vm#2 | 2 | 觀念
The enhanced second-chance algorithm classifies pages by (reference bit, dirty bit). Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Pages in class (0,0), not recently used and not modified, are replaced first.
[x] Class (0,1) is preferred over (1,0), since recent use matters more than saving to disk.
[ ] Pages in class (1,1) are replaced first, since they have been used the most.
[ ] Class (1,0) is preferred over (0,1), since a clean page need not be saved to disk first.
[ ] Pages in class (0,1) are never replaced, since they would have to be written back first.
---
「(0,0)」最近沒用也沒改過，換出不必寫回，最優先；「(0,1) 排在 (1,0) 前面」：最近沒用比較重要，寫回的代價次之。(1,1) 最後才換；(0,1) 只是要先寫回，當然可以被換。

@q vm2-14 | os-vm#2 | 2 | 找錯
When simulating FIFO, a student always replaces the page that was used most recently. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] The student's method is MRU, not FIFO.
[ ] The student's method is LRU, which replaces the page used longest ago.
[ ] The student's method is correct FIFO, since the newest page arrived last.
[ ] FIFO replaces the least recently used page, so the student is close.
[ ] FIFO replaces the page that will be used farthest in the future.
---
換掉「最近用過的」是「MRU」。FIFO 換的是最早**載入**的頁，跟最近有沒有用無關；換最久沒用的是 LRU，剛好和學生相反；換未來最晚用到的是 OPT。

@q vm2-15 | os-vm#2 | 3 | 情境
A database sequentially scans a table larger than its buffer pool, which uses LRU replacement. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Each page is used once and then pushed out, so the scan's hit rate is close to 0.
[x] The scan also pushes other frequently used pages out of the buffer.
[x] Databases often give such scans a small separate buffer.
[x] Using MRU for the scan keeps more of the other pages in the buffer.
[ ] LRU performs as well as OPT for a sequential scan, since every page is used exactly once.
---
循序掃描是 LRU 的最壞情況：「每頁用一次就被擠掉」，命中率接近 0，還「把其他常用的頁擠出去」。所以常「給掃描獨立的小緩衝區」，或「對掃描用 MRU」，只犧牲剛用過的掃描頁。OPT 知道未來，會留下之後還要用的頁，比 LRU 好得多。

@q vm2-16 | os-vm#2 | 2 | 比較
Which of the following statements about LFU page replacement (replace the page used the fewest times) are correct?
[x] A page used heavily early on but no longer used keeps a high count and stays in memory.
[x] Making the counts decay over time reduces this problem.
[x] LFU and LRU often choose different victims.
[ ] LFU cannot be implemented in practice, since its counters would overflow immediately.
[ ] LFU always chooses the same victim as LRU, since both of them keep track of past usage.
---
早期很熱、現在不用的頁「次數很高會一直留著」；「讓次數隨時間衰減」（例如定期右移）可以改善。LFU 看次數、LRU 看時間，「常常選不同的頁」。計數可以實作，只是要維護。

@q vm2-17 | os-vm#2 | 3 | 觀念
LRU and OPT have no Belady's anomaly because they are "stack algorithms." Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The set of pages in memory with $n$ frames is always a subset of the set with $n+1$ frames.
[x] This inclusion property guarantees that adding frames never increases the number of faults.
[x] FIFO does not have this property.
[x] That is why FIFO can show Belady's anomaly.
[x] The name refers to this inclusion property, not to an implementation with a stack.
---
「堆疊演算法」說的是「$n$ 個 frame 的頁一定包含在 $n+1$ 個裡」，所以「frame 變多不會多缺頁」；「FIFO 沒有這個性質」，「才會有 Belady 異常」。名稱「跟用堆疊實作無關」。五句都對。

@q vm2-18 | os-vm#2 | 2 | 計算
精確 LRU 用「計數器」實作：每次存取把目前時間寫進那一頁的頁表項。換頁時要做什麼？
[x] 找所有頁裡時間最小的：要掃整張表
[ ] 直接拿第一頁
[ ] 看堆疊頂端
[ ] 什麼都不用做
---
每次存取都要寫時間、換頁時要掃描，硬體成本太高，所以實務上用參考位元近似（Clock）。

@q vm2-19 | os-vm#2 | 2 | 情境
The operating system keeps a pool of free frames; on a fault it uses a frame from the pool immediately and chooses and writes back victims later. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] A faulting process does not wait for a dirty page to be written back, so it resumes sooner.
[x] A page placed in the pool can be taken back directly if it is needed again soon.
[ ] It reduces the number of page faults to 0, since a free frame is always ready for the next fault.
[ ] It removes the need for a replacement algorithm, since victims are chosen later anyway.
[ ] Its main purpose is to save memory by keeping fewer frames in use.
---
這叫 page buffering：缺頁時「直接拿空閒 frame，不必等寫回」；放進池裡的頁「很快又用到時還能拿回來」。缺頁照樣會發生；之後挑誰放進池裡還是要靠替換演算法；它其實多保留了一些 frame，不是為了省記憶體。

@q vm2-20 | os-vm#2 | 3 | 計算
參考串 4 7 0 7 1 0 1 2 1 2 7 1 2，3 個 frame。Clock（參考位元＋指針，載入時參考位元設 1）缺頁幾次？
== 6
---
程式模擬。這一串 Clock 跟 FIFO、LRU 一樣是 6 次。

@q vm3-08 | os-vm#3 | 2 | 計算
參考串 5 2 5 3 1 4 4 1 6 2 2 6 7，工作集視窗 $\Delta=4$。看完第 8 個參考時，工作集是？
== 1 4
== 1,4
---
最近 4 個參考是 1、4、4、1。雖然前面用了很多頁，最近只集中在兩頁。

@q vm3-09 | os-vm#3 | 3 | 計算
同一串，看完最後一個（第 13 個）時，$\Delta=4$ 和 $\Delta=6$ 的工作集各是？
== 2 6 7,1 2 6 7
?? Δ=4,Δ=6
---
最後 4 個：2 2 6 7；最後 6 個：1 6 2 2 6 7。視窗越大，工作集越大。

@q vm3-10 | os-vm#3 | 2 | 觀念
Page-fault-frequency control uses an upper limit of 10% and a lower limit of 2%. One process has a fault rate of 15% and another of 1%. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The process at 15% is given more frames.
[x] Some frames are taken away from the process at 1%.
[x] If no free frames are available for the 15% process, some process is suspended.
[x] A process with a fault rate of 5% would be left unchanged.
[ ] Frames are taken away from both processes, to lower the total fault rate of the whole system.
---
「高於上限多給」「低於下限收回」，「在兩者之間（5%）不動」；「沒有空 frame 可給就暫停某個行程」。從缺頁率 15% 的行程收回 frame 只會更糟。

@q vm3-11 | os-vm#3 | 2 | 情境
筆電 8 GB 記憶體，開了 50 個瀏覽器分頁加大型軟體，硬碟燈一直亮、每個動作都卡好幾秒。最可能的原因和處理？
[x] thrashing：所有程式的工作集加起來超過記憶體，一直在換頁；關掉一些程式
[ ] CPU 太慢
[ ] 硬碟壞了
[ ] 網路太慢
---
加 CPU 沒用，要減少同時在跑的工作或加記憶體。

@q vm3-12 | os-vm#3 | 3 | 陷阱
Can switching to local replacement (each process may replace only its own pages) completely eliminate thrashing? Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] No: it only keeps a thrashing process from taking other processes' frames.
[ ] Yes: it completely eliminates thrashing, since each process keeps its own frames.
[ ] It behaves exactly like global replacement.
[ ] It eliminates thrashing even when the total demand exceeds the available memory.
[ ] It makes the thrashing process stop faulting, since no one can take its frames away.
---
區域替換「只讓 thrashing 的行程不會去搶別人」，那個行程自己還是一直缺頁，大家排隊等磁碟也一樣變慢。它和全域替換確實不同；需求超過記憶體時無法消除。根本解法是給每個行程足夠的 frame，不夠就減少同時執行的行程。

@q vm3-13 | os-vm#3 | 2 | 計算
4 個行程的工作集大小 20、25、30、35，系統 100 個 frame。工作集模型會怎麼做？
[x] 總和 110 > 100，至少暫停一個行程，把它的 frame 給其他人
[ ] 全部執行
[ ] 每個行程給 25 個 frame
[ ] 把工作集最大的那個縮小
---
暫停後剩下的工作集加起來能放進記憶體，就不會 thrashing。

@q vm3-14 | os-vm#3 | 3 | 觀念
Which of the following statements about choosing the working-set window $\Delta$ are correct?
[x] If $\Delta$ is too small, the need for frames is underestimated.
[x] If $\Delta$ is too large, it spans several phases of the program, so frames are wasted.
[x] With an infinite $\Delta$, the working set is every page the program has ever used.
[ ] A larger $\Delta$ is always better, since it never misses a page that is in use.
[ ] A smaller $\Delta$ is always better, since it saves frames for other processes.
---
「太小」抓不到整個局部範圍，低估需要的 frame；「太大」跨好幾個階段，高估、浪費；「無限大」時就是用過的所有頁。所以兩個「越大／越小越好」都錯，要取中間。

@q vm3-15 | os-vm#3 | 3 | 計算
The working set is approximated with a timer: an interrupt occurs every 5000 references, and each page has its reference bit plus 2 history bits kept in memory. On each interrupt, the reference bit is shifted into the history and cleared. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] A page counts as in the working set if any of its three bits is 1.
[x] The approximate window covers about 10,000 to 15,000 references.
[ ] The window is exactly 5000 references, one timer interval.
[ ] The window is unbounded, since the history bits are never cleared.
[ ] Using fewer history bits would make the estimated window more accurate.
---
三個位元記錄最近三段，「任一個是 1 就算在工作集」；視窗涵蓋 2 到 3 個 5000 次的區間，「約 10000～15000 次」。歷史位元每次中斷都被推掉最舊的，視窗有界；位元越多、中斷越頻繁才越準。

@q vm3-16 | os-vm#3 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「工作集越大越好，代表程式用了更多記憶體、跑得越順。」錯在哪？
[x] 工作集是程式**需要**的頁數；它越大，系統能同時容納的行程越少，跟跑得順不順無關
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 工作集越小越容易 thrashing
[ ] 工作集跟記憶體無關
---
給的 frame 少於工作集才會頻繁缺頁；給更多不會更快。

@q vm3-17 | os-vm#3 | 2 | 比較
工作集模型和 PFF 防止 thrashing 的方式？
[x] 工作集：直接估每個行程需要幾頁；PFF：看缺頁率高低來增減 frame，比較直接也便宜
[ ] 兩者一樣
[ ] PFF 要記錄每次存取
[ ] 工作集只看缺頁率
---
兩者的目標相同：讓每個行程拿到「大概夠用」的 frame。

@q vm3-18 | os-vm#3 | 3 | 情境
畫「CPU 使用率 vs. 同時執行的行程數」的曲線，一開始上升、到某處突然暴跌。暴跌的原因？
[x] 行程太多，每個都拿不到足夠 frame，大家都在等換頁，CPU 反而閒著
[ ] CPU 過熱
[ ] 排程器壞了
[ ] 行程都結束了
---
如果排程器看到 CPU 閒就再加行程，會越加越糟。

@q vm4-07 | os-vm#4 | 3 | 計算
`int A[512][512]`（每個 int 4 bytes、頁 4 KB，每頁剛好放 2 列），整個陣列 256 頁。用「先列後行」(row-major) 和「先行後列」(column-major) 走訪各缺頁幾次？只給 1 個 frame、和給 256 個 frame 時呢？
== 256,131072,256,256
?? 列序1框,行序1框,列序256框,行序256框
---
列序：每頁用完再換，256。行序只有 1 個 frame：每走 2 列換頁，一行 256 次、512 行共 131072。frame 夠放全部時兩種都只有強制缺頁 256。

@q vm4-08 | os-vm#4 | 2 | 計算
工作集 1 GB，要讓 TLB 能涵蓋全部，4 KB 頁和 2 MB 頁各需要幾個 TLB 項？
== 262144,512
?? 4KB,2MB
---
$2^{30}/2^{12}$ 和 $2^{30}/2^{21}$。大頁讓 TLB reach 大很多，代價是內部碎片。

@q vm4-09 | os-vm#4 | 2 | 計算
行程有 100 頁，fork 之後子行程只寫了其中 3 頁，父行程沒寫。用 copy-on-write 時實際複製了幾頁？
== 3
---
寫入時才複製被寫的那頁，其他 97 頁父子共用。如果子行程接著 exec，連這 3 頁的複製都可能不會發生。

@q vm4-10 | os-vm#4 | 3 | 陷阱
A page is shared copy-on-write between a parent and its child, and the **parent** writes to it first. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] The parent, which writes first, gets a new copy of the page.
[ ] The child always gets the new copy, whoever writes first.
[ ] The parent modifies the shared page directly, without any copying.
[ ] Both processes immediately get new copies of the page.
[ ] The write fails, since copy-on-write pages stay read-only forever.
---
COW 對父子對稱：「誰先寫誰就拿到新複本」，原本那頁留給還沒寫的一方，所以兩邊不必都複製。直接改共用頁會讓子行程看到，違反 fork 的語意；唯讀只是用來觸發複製，寫入照樣會成功。

@q vm4-11 | os-vm#4 | 3 | 情境
Redis uses `fork` so that the child writes a memory snapshot to disk while the parent keeps serving requests. The workload has many writes. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Memory use can surge, because every page the parent modifies is copied.
[x] In the worst case, memory use approaches twice the size of the data.
[x] Spare memory should be reserved when deploying it.
[ ] Memory use stays unchanged, since copy-on-write never copies anything after the fork.
[ ] Memory use drops by half, since parent and child share all pages.
---
COW 平時省記憶體，但「父行程每改一頁就複製一頁」，大量寫入時「最壞接近兩倍」，部署時「要預留空間」。有寫入就會複製；共用只是不增加，不會減半。

@q vm4-12 | os-vm#4 | 2 | 計算
buddy system 從 1 MB 配置 70 KB。實際給多大？要切幾次？
== 128,3
?? 給多少KB,切幾次
---
1 MB → 512 → 256 → 128 KB，切 3 次，給 128 KB（內部碎片 58 KB）。

@q vm4-13 | os-vm#4 | 3 | 追蹤
buddy system 1 MB，依序配置 A 60 KB、B 200 KB、C 30 KB（都沒釋放）。剩下的空閒區塊大小有哪些（KB）？
== 32 128 512
== 32,128,512
---
A：1M→512+512，512→256+256，256→128+128，128→64+64，A 拿 64。B：要 256，用剩下那個 256。C：要 32，把 A 旁邊的 64 切成 32+32，拿一個。空閒：32、128、512。

@q vm4-14 | os-vm#4 | 2 | 觀念
Which of the following statements about the slab allocator are correct?
[x] Each kind of kernel object has its own set of pre-cut slots.
[x] Allocating and freeing slots is fast, with little fragmentation for that object size.
[x] Freed objects can stay initialized, ready for reuse.
[x] Linux uses a buddy system for large blocks and slabs on top of it for small objects.
[ ] Every slab request is rounded up to the next power of 2, just as in the buddy system.
---
「每種核心物件一組事先切好的格子」，「配置釋放快、沒有大小不合的碎片」，「釋放後可保持初始化」；Linux「buddy 管大塊、slab 在上面管小物件」。捨入到 2 的冪次是 buddy 的做法。

@q vm4-15 | os-vm#4 | 2 | 計算
行程被換回時預先載入它之前的 100 頁，但其中只有 30 頁真的會被用到。這次 prepaging 省下幾次缺頁、浪費幾頁的載入？
== 30,70
?? 省下,浪費
---
用到的比例 $\alpha$ 接近 1 才划算。

@q vm4-16 | os-vm#4 | 2 | 情境
A file is mapped into memory with `mmap` and then read and written directly through pointers. Which of the following statements describe what happens?
[x] The file's contents are mapped page by page into the virtual address space.
[x] The first touch of a page causes a page fault that reads that page from the file.
[ ] The whole file is read into memory as soon as `mmap` is called.
[ ] Every access through the pointer calls `read` or `write` on the file behind the scenes.
[ ] Modified pages are never written back, so all changes are lost at exit.
---
檔案「按頁對到虛擬記憶體」，「第一次碰到某頁就缺頁、從檔案讀進來」。不是呼叫時就整個讀；之後是一般的記憶體存取，不經過 `read`／`write`；共享映射改過的頁之後會寫回檔案。

@q vm4-17 | os-vm#4 | 3 | 比較
`vfork` lets the child borrow the parent's address space while the parent is suspended. Compare it with `fork` using copy-on-write. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] `vfork` does not even copy the page table, so it is faster.
[x] Changes made by the child are visible to the parent, so `vfork` suits only a child that calls `exec` at once.
[x] With copy-on-write `fork`, copying the page tables still costs time.
[ ] `vfork` is safer than `fork`, since the parent is suspended while the child runs.
[ ] `fork` with copy-on-write still copies all data pages immediately.
---
vfork「連頁表都不複製」，更快，但「子行程改的父行程看得到」，只適合馬上 exec，反而比較不安全。COW 的 fork 只複製頁表、寫到才複製頁，「頁表本身在超大行程上也有成本」。

@q vm4-18 | os-vm#4 | 2 | 找錯
A student says: "Larger pages are always better: TLB reach grows and page faults become fewer." Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Internal fragmentation grows with larger pages.
[x] Each fault moves more data, possibly including data that is never used.
[x] Systems therefore often support both normal pages and huge pages.
[x] Larger pages do enlarge TLB reach.
[ ] Larger pages always increase the number of page faults, since each page holds more data.
---
大頁「確實讓 TLB reach 變大」、缺頁通常變少，但代價是「內部碎片變大」、「每次缺頁搬更多、可能載入用不到的資料」，所以「系統常同時支援一般頁和大頁」。大頁不會讓缺頁一定變多。

@q st1-10 | os-storage#1 | 2 | 計算
磁頭在 100（磁柱 0–199），請求序列 23、89、132、42、187、150、5、120。FCFS 和 SSTF 的總移動量？
== 718,291
?? FCFS,SSTF
---
FCFS 照順序：77+66+43+90+145+37+145+115。SSTF：89、120、132、150、187、42、23、5，$11+31+12+18+37+145+19+18$。程式驗算。

@q st1-11 | os-storage#1 | 3 | 陷阱
同一組（磁頭 100；23、89、132、42、187、150、5、120），LOOK 先往大的方向走，總移動量 269，比 SSTF 的 291 還少。這說明什麼？
[x] SSTF 每步只挑眼前最近的，不保證總移動量最小
[ ] 程式算錯了，SSTF 一定最小
[ ] LOOK 永遠比 SSTF 好
[ ] 總移動量跟演算法無關
---
SSTF 一開始往 89 走（只差 11），結果後面要從 187 一路走回 5；LOOK 先把大的那邊掃完只要回頭一次。

@q st1-12 | os-storage#1 | 2 | 計算
同一組，SCAN 先往 0 的方向（走到 0 再回頭）和 LOOK 先往小的方向，總移動量各是？
== 287,277
?? SCAN,LOOK
---
SCAN：$100+187$（100→0、0→187）；LOOK：$95+182$（100→5、5→187）。LOOK 不用走到盡頭。

@q st1-13 | os-storage#1 | 3 | 計算
同一組，C-LOOK 往大的方向：服務完 187 後直接跳到最小的請求 5 再往上。跳回去的距離也算的話，總移動量？
== 353
---
$87$（100→187）＋$182$（187→5）＋$84$（5→89）。C-LOOK 讓每個請求的等待時間比較平均，總移動量不一定少。程式驗算。

@q st1-14 | os-storage#1 | 3 | 情境
Each request to a hard disk pays 5 ms of seek time plus 4 ms of rotational delay, followed by transfer at 100 MB/s, where 1 MB is $10^6$ bytes. A backup program must read 1 MiB of data. If the file is stored contiguously, the program issues one 1 MiB request; if the file is fragmented, it must issue 256 independent random 4 KiB requests. Every random request pays its own seek and rotational delay, the requests are served one at a time, and nothing is cached. The program's author wants to know how much the fragmentation costs. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Both cases transfer the same total amount of data.
[x] The contiguous request takes approximately 19.49 ms.
[x] The random requests together take approximately 2314.49 ms.
[ ] The contiguous request takes only 10.49 ms including the stated mechanical overhead.
[ ] The 256 independent random requests together pay only one 9 ms overhead.
---
「資料量一樣」對：256×4 KiB＝1 MiB。「19.49 ms」對：傳輸 1 MiB 需約 10.48576 ms，再加 9。「2314.49 ms」對：256×9＋10.48576＝2314.48576。「只花 10.49」錯：那是傳輸本身，漏掉 9 ms。「只付一次」錯：題設每個獨立隨機請求都付一次，不可套用連續單筆的成本。

@q st1-15 | os-storage#1 | 2 | 計算
10000 RPM 的硬碟，平均尋軌 4 ms。只考慮尋軌和旋轉延遲，每秒大約能做幾次隨機讀取（IOPS）？
== 142
== 143
---
一圈 6 ms，平均旋轉延遲 3 ms，每次約 7 ms，$1000/7\approx142$。固態硬碟可以到幾十萬。

@q st1-16 | os-storage#1 | 2 | 找錯
學生說「7200 RPM 的平均旋轉延遲是 8.33 ms」。錯在哪？
[x] 8.33 ms 是轉一整圈的時間；平均只要等半圈，約 4.17 ms
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 應該是 7.2 ms
[ ] 應該是 60 ms
---
$60/7200$ 秒＝8.33 ms 一圈。

@q st1-17 | os-storage#1 | 2 | 觀念
快閃記憶體的固態硬碟不能「直接覆寫」一頁。改一頁的內容實際上怎麼做？
[x] 寫到另一個已經抹除過的空頁，把舊頁標成無效；抹除要以比頁大很多的區塊為單位，之後由垃圾回收整理
[ ] 直接覆寫
[ ] 先抹除那一頁再寫
[ ] 不能修改
---
所以會有寫入放大：為了回收一個區塊，要把裡面還有效的頁搬走再抹除。

@q st1-18 | os-storage#1 | 3 | 觀念
An SSD controller must erase a flash block before rewriting it, and in this simplified model each block is rated for 1000 erase cycles. Two blocks, X and Y, are currently free:

| block | erase cycles so far |
|---|---|
| X | 10 |
| Y | 990 |

The controller must perform one new erase to store an updated copy of a logical page. It chooses the less-worn block and updates its mapping table, which records the physical block that holds each logical page, so that later reads of the page find the new copy; the old copy is then marked as invalid. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The chosen block's erase count becomes 11.
[x] Spreading repeated erases can avoid concentrating all wear on one block.
[x] Changing physical placement must preserve the mapping used to read the logical data.
[ ] Wear leveling makes the specified endurance threshold infinite.
[ ] One new erase makes the less-worn block's count reach 1000.
---
「11 次」對：10＋1。「避免磨損集中」對：若一直只用同一區塊，它會較早接近限制。「仍需正確映射」對：資料換實體位置後不能讓讀取指到錯處。「壽命無限」錯：平衡使用不等於消除抹寫限制。「一次到 1000」錯：剩餘差距為 990 次，不是一個新抹除就用完。

@q st1-19 | os-storage#1 | 3 | 情境
In a simplified SSD, garbage collection copies every page it believes is still needed before erasing a 4-page block. Of its pages, 2 are live and 2 belong to deleted files. Without a discard notification, the SSD still treats all four as needed; with an honored TRIM notification, it knows the latter two are unused. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Under this model, TRIM reduces the required copied pages from four to two.
[x] Informing the SSD that data are unused can avoid copying those data during reclamation.
[ ] TRIM must overwrite each notified page immediately with zeros.
[x] Removing a filesystem name and notifying a storage device of unused ranges are different operations.
[ ] The model requires copying zero pages after TRIM even though two live pages remain.
---
「四變二」對：未告知時四頁都被當作有效；告知後只需搬兩頁真正活資料。「省無用搬運」對：這是減少回收工作的一個原因。「立刻清零」錯：discard 是告知範圍不再需要，不等於此處保證立即安全抹除。「刪名字與告知裝置不同」對：裝置不能只憑檔案名稱判斷哪些實體資料還要保留。「不用搬任何頁」錯：仍需保存兩頁活資料。

@q st1-20 | os-storage#1 | 2 | 比較
NVMe 固態硬碟約 50 萬 IOPS，傳統硬碟約 150 IOPS。對作業系統的 I/O 排程有什麼影響？
[x] 固態硬碟沒有尋軌，排序請求省不了多少時間，排程器反而變成負擔；通常用簡單的 FIFO 或直接送多個佇列
[ ] 要用更複雜的 SCAN
[ ] 沒影響
[ ] 固態硬碟需要 SSTF
---
現代 Linux 對 NVMe 預設常用 none 或很簡單的排程器。

@q st1-21 | os-storage#1 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼 SSTF 可能讓某些請求飢餓。
---
SSTF 每次挑離目前磁頭最近的請求。若磁頭附近一直有新請求進來，磁頭就一直在那一帶來回服務，遠處的請求永遠不會是「最近的」，可以無限期等下去。SCAN 類演算法每一趟都會掃過整個範圍，所以等待時間有上限。

@q st2-09 | os-storage#2 | 3 | 計算
RAID 5 更新一個資料區塊：舊資料 1010、新資料 0110、舊同位元 1100。新的同位元？
== 0000
---
新同位元＝舊同位元 ⊕ 舊資料 ⊕ 新資料＝1100⊕1010⊕0110。這就是小寫入要「讀舊資料、讀舊同位元、寫新資料、寫新同位元」4 次 I/O 的原因。

@q st2-10 | os-storage#2 | 3 | 計算
4 顆硬碟。RAID 1+0（兩兩鏡像、再條帶）在「任意壞 2 顆」的 6 種情況中，幾種不掉資料？RAID 0+1（兩兩條帶、再鏡像）呢？
== 4,2
?? RAID 1+0,RAID 0+1
---
1+0：只有剛好壞同一對鏡像的 2 種會掉，其他 4 種沒事。0+1：壞一顆就讓那一整組條帶失效，第二顆壞在另一組就全掉；只有兩顆都在同一組的 2 種沒事。

@q st2-11 | os-storage#2 | 3 | 計算
4 顆硬碟各能做 100 次隨機 I/O／秒，全部用來做隨機小寫入。RAID 5 和 RAID 10 各能撐多少次寫入／秒？
== 100,200
?? RAID 5,RAID 10
---
總共 400 次 I/O。RAID 5 每次小寫入要 4 次 I/O；RAID 10 要 2 次（寫兩份鏡像）。

@q st2-12 | os-storage#2 | 2 | 計算
4 顆硬碟的 RAID 5，其中一顆壞了（降級模式）。要讀原本在壞硬碟上的那一塊，要讀幾次？
== 3
---
讀同一條 stripe 在其他 3 顆上的區塊（含同位元），XOR 起來就是遺失的那塊。降級時讀取變慢。

@q st2-13 | os-storage#2 | 3 | 比較
RAID 5 一次寫入整條 stripe（4 顆資料＋1 顆同位元，寫滿 4 個資料區塊）。要幾次 I/O？跟小寫入比？
[x] 5 次寫（同位元直接從新資料算出，不用先讀）；平均每個資料區塊 1.25 次，比小寫入的 4 次好很多
[ ] 16 次
[ ] 4 次
[ ] 8 次
---
所以 RAID 5 適合大量循序寫入，怕零星的小寫入。

@q st2-14 | os-storage#2 | 3 | 情境
A twelve-disk RAID 5 uses 16 TB disks. After one complete disk failure, a specified rebuild reads all 16 TB from each of the eleven surviving disks. Total read throughput across all survivors is 1 TB per hour, with all other costs ignored. No rebuild has completed when considering another disk failure. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The specified rebuild reads 176 TB in total.
[x] The read phase requires 176 hours at the stated total rate.
[x] A second complete disk failure exceeds the array's one-disk redundancy guarantee before repair completes.
[ ] Only 16 TB need be read in total under the stated read-all-survivors rule.
[ ] Having twelve disks makes RAID 5 automatically tolerate any two complete disk failures.
---
「176 TB」對：11×16。「176 小時」對：176÷1，約 7.33 天，只依題設的聚合速率計算。「第二顆超過保護」對：RAID 5 是單顆磁碟故障的冗餘，尚未修復時又壞一顆便超出保證。「只讀 16」錯：題設要讀十一顆的全部內容。「十二顆就耐任意兩顆」錯：碟數多不會把單同位元變成雙重保護；這不等於宣稱故障會抹掉所有存活磁碟上的每個位元組。

@q st2-15 | os-storage#2 | 3 | 陷阱
A video editor builds a RAID 0 array from four 1 GiB disks to get the highest read and write speed; there is no other copy, parity, or backup. The project files are striped across all four disks in 64 KiB stripe units, so every large file is spread over every disk. One day disk 3 fails completely, while the other three disks keep their contents and still work. The editor asks a technician how much space the array had offered and what can still be recovered from the three surviving disks. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The original usable data space is 4 GiB, ignoring metadata.
[x] RAID 0 itself provides no redundant copy from which to restore the missing disk's stripes.
[ ] The four disks automatically contain mirrored copies merely because several disks participate.
[x] Some data can remain physically present on the surviving disks even though the full dataset is no longer available.
[ ] Four 1 GiB disks provide 8 GiB of usable RAID 0 data space.
---
「4 GiB」對：四顆各一 GiB，條帶化不額外複製。「無冗餘可補缺塊」對：RAID 0 本身沒有鏡像或同位元保護。「自動多份鏡像」錯：碟數多不等於有副本。「其他碟仍可有資料」對：一碟故障不會魔法般清掉另外三碟，但完整資料集已缺部分。「8 GiB」錯：總原始空間只有四 GiB。

@q st2-16 | os-storage#2 | 3 | 觀念
A two-disk mirror keeps identical data on both disks, and each disk reads at 100 MiB/s. Two independent 8 MiB read requests both arrive at time 0. The controller can send one request to each disk so that they are served concurrently, or it can send both to the same disk, which then serves them one after the other. Each individual request is always served entirely by the one disk it is assigned to, and is never split between the disks. Ignore seek time, rotational delay, and every other cost. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Each request takes 80 ms on its assigned disk.
[x] Both concurrent requests can finish in 80 ms in this model.
[x] Serving the two requests serially on one disk takes 160 ms.
[ ] One 8 MiB request automatically takes 40 ms solely because a second full copy exists, despite the stated one-disk-per-request rule.
[ ] These assumptions prove that every real mirrored workload always has exactly twice a single disk's performance.
---
「每筆 80 ms」對：8÷100 秒＝0.08 秒。「一起 80 ms」對：兩顆各做一筆，同時進行。「串行 160 ms」對：兩個 80 相加。「單筆自然 40 ms」錯：題設每筆只由被分派的一顆讀取，沒有把單筆拆成兩半。「永遠正好兩倍」錯：這是展示讀取分流的可能好處，實際效果還要看工作負載與控制器，不能推成所有情況的固定倍數。

@q st2-17 | os-storage#2 | 2 | 找錯
學生說「5 顆 2 TB 的 RAID 5，可用容量是一半，5 TB」。正確是？
== 8
---
RAID 5 只拿一顆的容量放同位元：$(5-1)\times2$ TB。一半是 RAID 1／10 的情況。

@q st2-18 | os-storage#2 | 3 | 觀念
Four active 3 GiB disks form two mirrored pairs that are striped together. A fifth 3 GiB disk is a configured hot spare and stores no ordinary array data before a failure. A rebuild must copy 3 GiB from surviving mirror data at a sustained 64 MiB/s, with no other costs. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The active mirrored organization provides 6 GiB of usable data space before the failure.
[x] The spare can be assigned to replace a failed member under the stated configuration.
[x] The stated rebuild requires 48 seconds.
[ ] Merely having a hot spare means all lost redundancy is restored instantaneously after a failure.
[ ] All five disks provide 15 GiB of ordinary usable array data before the failure.
---
「6 GiB」對：兩組鏡像各提供一顆 3 GiB 的資料量。「可接替失敗成員」對：這是已配置熱備援的用途。「48 秒」對：3 GiB＝3072 MiB，3072÷64＝48。「立即完成」錯：仍需重建資料，期間保護程度尚未完全恢復。「15 GiB」錯：忽略了鏡像重複資料與尚未投入的備援碟。

@q st2-19 | os-storage#2 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼 RAID 5 用 XOR 當同位元，就能在壞任何一顆硬碟時還原資料。
---
同位元 $P=D_1\oplus D_2\oplus\cdots\oplus D_k$。XOR 滿足 $x\oplus x=0$、交換結合律。壞的若是 $D_i$，把其他資料和 $P$ 全部 XOR：$P\oplus\bigoplus_{j\ne i}D_j=D_i$（其他的都成對抵消）。壞的若是同位元那顆，重新算一次 $P$ 即可。壞兩顆時方程不夠，所以只能容忍一顆。

@q st3-07 | os-storage#3 | 2 | 計算
inode 有 10 個直接指標、1 個單層間接、1 個雙層間接；區塊 1 KB、指標 4 bytes。最大檔案多少 KB？
== 65802
---
每個間接區塊放 $1024/4=256$ 個指標：$10+256+256^2=65802$ 個區塊，每塊 1 KB。

@q st3-08 | os-storage#3 | 2 | 計算
同上（10 直接、256 個指標／間接區塊），inode 已在記憶體。讀第 300 個區塊（從 0 起算）要幾次磁碟 I/O？
== 3
---
0～9 直接、10～265 單層間接、266 起雙層。300 在雙層：讀雙層間接區塊、單層間接區塊、資料區塊。

@q st3-09 | os-storage#3 | 3 | 計算
12 個直接、每個間接區塊 1024 個指標，inode **不在**記憶體。讀第 5000 個區塊（從 0 起算）要幾次 I/O？
== 4
---
$12+1024=1036\le5000$，在雙層間接範圍。inode 1 次＋雙層 1＋單層 1＋資料 1。

@q st3-10 | os-storage#3 | 2 | 計算
鏈結配置，檔案的紀錄只存第一塊在哪。要讀第 50 個區塊（從 0 起算），最少幾次磁碟讀取？
== 51
---
要從第 0 塊一路沿指標讀到第 50 塊。這就是鏈結配置隨機存取慢的原因。

@q st3-11 | os-storage#3 | 3 | 觀念
A file is stored with FAT chains: the allocation table has one entry per cluster, holding the number of the next cluster of the same file. Logical cluster indices start at 0, the address of the file's first cluster is known, the complete table is cached in memory, and no file-data clusters are cached. There is no additional per-file index of positions. A video player wants to jump to the middle of a file and read logical cluster 50:
```
c = first_cluster
repeat 50 times: c = FAT[c]     // in memory
read cluster c from disk
```
Reading one cluster from the stick takes about 0.5 ms, while one table lookup in memory takes about 10 ns. The player's developers want to know how many disk reads the jump costs and where the time goes. To obtain the contents of logical cluster 50, which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Following fifty in-memory next entries reaches logical cluster 50.
[x] Only one file-data-cluster disk read is then needed for the requested contents.
[x] Earlier data clusters need not be read from disk just to retrieve their next addresses.
[ ] The target contents require zero disk reads solely because the FAT is cached.
[ ] The described lookup necessarily performs 51 data-cluster disk reads.
---
「五十次記憶體跳轉」對：起點是索引零，要前進五十步。「資料只讀一次」對：到達目標地址後讀那一塊。「不需讀前面資料塊」對：鏈結都在快取的 FAT 裡。「磁碟零次」錯：快取的是表，題設沒有目標資料。「51 次資料讀取」錯：那是把表內走訪誤當成逐塊磁碟讀取。

@q st3-12 | os-storage#3 | 2 | 計算
32 GB 的磁碟、4 KB 的 cluster，FAT32 每項 4 bytes。FAT 表多大（MB）？
== 32
---
$2^{35}/2^{12}=2^{23}$ 項 × 4 bytes $=2^{25}$ bytes。表大到記憶體放不下時，FAT 的優勢就消失了。

@q st3-13 | os-storage#3 | 2 | 計算
連續配置，檔案從磁碟第 100 塊開始、長 20 塊，區塊 4 KB。檔案的第 30000 個位元組在磁碟哪一塊、塊內第幾個位元組？
== 107,1328
?? 磁碟區塊,塊內位置
---
$30000=7\times4096+1328$，檔案第 7 塊＝磁碟 $100+7$。連續配置的隨機存取只要一次加法。

@q st3-14 | os-storage#3 | 3 | 陷阱
鏈結配置中，某一塊的「下一塊指標」壞掉了，後果？
[x] 那一塊之後的整段檔案都找不到了
[ ] 只掉那一塊
[ ] 沒影響
[ ] 整個磁碟都壞
---
索引配置的指標集中在 inode／索引塊，比較好保護（例如多存一份）。

@q st3-15 | os-storage#3 | 2 | 比較
連續配置要求檔案事先知道大小，而且檔案長大時可能放不下。現代檔案系統（例如 ext4）常用的折衷？
[x] extent：用（起點, 長度）記錄一段段連續的區塊，大檔案只要幾個 extent
[ ] 全部改用鏈結
[ ] 每個檔案固定 1 GB
[ ] 不讓檔案長大
---
大部分檔案只需要少數幾段，循序讀寫接近連續配置的速度。

@q st3-16 | os-storage#3 | 3 | 找錯
A file system with 12 direct pointers per inode reads a file's logical data block 0, which is addressed by one of the inode's direct pointers. The inode's disk location is already known from an earlier lookup, but neither the inode nor the target data block is in the cache, and reading either one takes one disk-block I/O of about 5 ms. All other metadata can be ignored. A student traces the access step by step and compares it with an access to logical block 20, which would go through the single-indirect block. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Reading the inode and then the data requires two disk-block reads.
[x] If the inode were already cached, only the target data-block read would remain.
[x] This direct-pointer access does not require reading a single-indirect block.
[ ] The uncached inode costs no I/O merely because its location is known.
[ ] Logical block 0 must always pass through a single-indirect block before its data can be read.
---
「兩次」對：知道 inode 在哪裡，不等於它的內容已在記憶體；先讀 inode，再按直接指標讀資料。「inode 已快取只剩一次」對。「不走單層間接」對：題設區塊零使用直接指標。「知道位置就不用讀」錯：位置與內容是兩回事。「一定走間接」錯：那會多算根本不需要的查詢。

@q st3-17 | os-storage#3 | 3 | 證明
inode 有 $d$ 個直接指標、一個單層和一個雙層間接，區塊 $B$ bytes、指標 $p$ bytes。寫出最大檔案大小並說明。
---
每個間接區塊可放 $n=B/p$ 個指標。直接指標指到 $d$ 塊；單層間接區塊裡的 $n$ 個指標各指一塊；雙層間接區塊有 $n$ 個指標，各指到一個單層間接區塊，再各有 $n$ 塊，共 $n^2$。最大檔案 $(d+n+n^2)\cdot B$ bytes（若有三層再加 $n^3$）。

@q st3-18 | os-storage#3 | 3 | 情境
A database file has 100 data blocks and needs random reads as well as later growth. Compare simple contiguous allocation, embedded-pointer linked allocation without an auxiliary index, and an indexed layout whose index can address all 100 blocks. The physical block immediately after a contiguous file is occupied by another live file. Each block is 4 KiB. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The contiguous file cannot simply occupy that next block to grow without moving while the other file retains it.
[x] The linked layout may require traversing earlier blocks to find a randomly selected later block.
[x] An indexed layout can map nonconsecutive data blocks without requiring the whole file to be one physical run.
[ ] A linked layout gains direct random indexing merely because appending another block is convenient.
[ ] Every indexed layout stores zero index metadata regardless of file size.
---
「不能硬占下一塊」對：它已屬於別人的活檔，成長需另找位置或採其他安排。「鏈結隨機讀需走訪」對：未提供額外索引就得沿鏈找。「索引可映射分散塊」對：各項記錄目標位置。「好追加就能直接隨機找」錯：成長容易與隨機定位成本是不同取捨。「索引零成本」錯：索引本身需要 metadata，不能忽略所有規模下的空間成本。

@q st4-06 | os-storage#4 | 2 | 計算
bitmap 從第 0 塊開始是 1111 0011 1010（1＝已使用、0＝空閒）。第一個空閒的區塊編號？
== 4
---
從左數第 0～3 都是 1，第 4 個是 0。用硬體「找第一個 0 位元」的指令可以很快找到。

@q st4-07 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 觀念
Free space is represented by two records: start block 10 with length 100, and start block 1000 with length 50. A record represents exactly its stated number of consecutive blocks. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The records describe 150 free blocks in total.
[x] The first record describes blocks 10 through 109 inclusive.
[ ] The first record includes block 110.
[x] Long consecutive free runs can be compactly represented by a small number of start/length records.
[ ] All 150 described blocks form one consecutive free run.
---
「150 塊」對：100＋50。「10 到 109」對：從 10 起算 100 個，最後是 10＋100−1。「包括 110」錯：那會變成 101 個。「長段可少量描述」對：這是計數法適合集中空間的原因。「全部連成一段」錯：第一段到 109，第二段從 1000 開始，中間有間隔。

@q st4-08 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 陷阱
Ignoring the special entries `.` and `..`, directory p contains directory q. Suppose an arbitrary additional hard link inside q is allowed to refer back to p. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The added directory reference creates the cycle p to q to p.
[x] Unrestricted recursive traversal would need a way to detect repeated directory objects.
[ ] The path p to q to p returns to p after three directory edges.
[ ] A directory hard link necessarily copies the entire target directory tree.
[ ] Symbolic links are inherently incapable of forming cycles during pathname resolution.
---
「形成環」對：從 p 到 q，又能回 p。「需要辨識重複物件」對：否則盲目遞迴可能不停止。「三條邊」錯：p 到 q 一條，q 回 p 一條，共兩條。「複製整棵目錄」錯：硬連結增加參照，不是複製內容。「符號連結不能形成環」錯：它也能形成循環路徑，需要適當限制與偵測。限制任意目錄硬連結可簡化目錄結構與管理。

@q st4-09 | os-storage#4 | 2 | 計算
建立檔案 f（連結數 1），`ln f g`，`ln -s f h`，再 `rm f`。最後 inode 的連結數？`cat h` 會怎樣？
== 1,失敗
?? 連結數,cat h
---
hard link g 讓連結數變 2；symbolic link 不算；刪 f 後剩 1（資料還在，透過 g 能讀）。h 存的是路徑 f，f 已不存在，變成懸空連結。

@q st4-10 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 追蹤
父行程開了一個檔案後 fork。父行程讀 10 個位元組，接著子行程也讀 10 個位元組。子行程讀到的是檔案第幾到第幾個位元組（從 0 起算）？
== 10,19
?? 起,迄
---
fork 後父子的檔案描述子指向系統開檔表的**同一個**項目，共用讀寫位置。如果是兩個行程各自 open，就各有各的位置。

@q st4-11 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 觀念
Two independent `open` calls on the same regular file create descriptions x and y, both at position 0. A read through x consumes 3 bytes, and a read through y consumes 2. Then `dup` creates descriptor z referring to x's open description, and a read through z consumes 4 bytes. Assume every read returns the requested amount. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Before the read through z, x's position is 3 and y's is 2.
[x] The duplicated descriptor z shares x's file position.
[x] After the read through z, x's position is 7 while y's remains 2.
[ ] Every descriptor must have an independent position, including descriptors created by `dup`.
[ ] The read through z advances y's position from 2 to 7.
---
「原來 3 與 2」對：兩次獨立 open 建立不同的開啟描述。「z 共用 x 位置」對：dup 指向同一個開啟描述。「之後 x 為 7、y 仍 2」對：3＋4＝7，另一個描述不受影響。「每描述元都獨立」錯：要分清描述元與開啟描述物件。「y 也到 7」錯：它不是經 dup 共用的那一個。

@q st4-12 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 情境
A process keeps an open descriptor for a 10 GiB log. The last name is unlinked, then a new independent 1 GiB file is created at the same pathname. The old descriptor remains open. Ignore snapshots, compression, and metadata. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The old descriptor still refers to the old file rather than automatically switching to the new one.
[x] The two files can together retain 11 GiB of data space at this point.
[ ] Reusing the pathname makes the old descriptor refer to the new file automatically.
[x] Closing the final reference to the old file can permit reclaiming its data without deleting the new file.
[ ] The old 10 GiB must already be reclaimed solely because its pathname was reused.
---
「舊描述元仍指舊檔」對：開啟參照追蹤的是物件，不是每次重新解析名字。「共 11 GiB」對：舊 10 尚被參照，新 1 另占空間。「同名自動改指」錯：新檔是不同物件。「關完舊參照可回收舊資料」對：新檔仍有自己的名字與資料。「重用名字就回收」錯：不能越過仍活著的開啟參照。

@q st4-13 | os-storage#4 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「刪掉 symbolic link，它指到的檔案也會被刪。」錯在哪？
[x] 刪除的是連結本身（一個存著路徑的小檔案），目標檔案不受影響
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 兩個都會被刪
[ ] 不能刪 symbolic link
---
反過來，刪掉目標，symbolic link 會變成懸空。

@q st4-14 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 比較
Two mounted ordinary POSIX filesystems each contain an unrelated inode numbered 7. Both filesystems happen to reside on the same physical disk. Consider normal hard links and symbolic links. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Equal inode numbers in different filesystems do not by themselves identify the same file object.
[x] A symbolic-link pathname can resolve to a file in the other mounted filesystem.
[x] Normal hard-link identity is confined to one filesystem.
[ ] Sharing one physical disk makes cross-filesystem hard links valid automatically.
[ ] The two inode-7 objects must be the same file because their numeric inode identifiers match.
---
「數字相同不等於同檔」對：inode 編號的識別範圍是檔案系統。「符號連結可跨掛載」對：它保存並解析路徑。「硬連結限同檔案系統」對：它增加的是該系統內相同物件的名字。「同一實體碟就可跨」錯：限制依檔案系統邊界，不是磁碟顆數。「兩個 7 必同檔」錯：需要把所屬檔案系統一起納入辨識。

@q st4-15 | os-storage#4 | 2 | 計算
3 個行程開了同一個檔案（各自 open），系統開檔表中這個 inode 的開啟計數是多少？其中一個關檔後呢？
== 3,2
?? 計數,關一個後
---
計數歸零才把 inode 從記憶體的表中移除。

@q st4-16 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 觀念
A grouping-based free-space structure uses 4096-byte management blocks. One block contains 511 available-block addresses and one pointer to the next management block, each eight bytes. Only the 511 listed data-block addresses are offered by this step. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The 512 stored entries exactly fill 4096 bytes.
[x] One management-block read can provide 511 available data-block addresses.
[x] Following the final pointer can obtain another batch of addresses.
[ ] The same block can hold 512 available addresses plus the extra eight-byte next pointer without exceeding 4096 bytes.
[ ] This scheme stores only one availability bit per volume block, just like a bitmap.
---
「剛好 4096」對：(511＋1)×8。「一次得 511 個地址」對：題設這一步列出這些可用資料塊。「末項接下一批」對：不必讓每個可用地址各自只告訴你下一個地址。「512 個再加一個也放得下」錯：513×8＝4104，超出一塊。「等同位元圖」錯：這裡存的是地址批次，不是每塊一個位元。

@q st4-17 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼 hard link 的刪除要用「連結數」而不能一刪名字就釋放資料。
---
一個 inode 可以有多個目錄項目指向它（多個名字）。若刪除任一個名字就釋放資料，其他名字會指向已被回收、可能被別的檔案重用的區塊，造成資料錯亂。用連結數記錄還有幾個名字，刪名字只減 1，歸零（而且沒人開著）才真正釋放 inode 和資料區塊。

@q st5-06 | os-storage#5 | 2 | 追蹤
日誌式檔案系統寫入一筆交易的順序？①把修改寫進日誌 ②寫入 commit 記錄 ③把修改寫到原本的位置（checkpoint）④釋放日誌空間
== 1 2 3 4
== 1,2,3,4
---
commit 寫完才算交易成立；之後才能動原位置；原位置寫完日誌才能回收。

@q st5-07 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 觀念
A redo-journal model writes a transaction's changes into the log, durably commits it, and only then may update home locations. A crash occurs after its log data are written but before any valid commit record exists. No home location has been changed. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Recovery should not replay this uncommitted transaction.
[x] Ignoring its incomplete log preserves the unchanged home state in this scenario.
[ ] Log data alone prove that the transaction committed successfully.
[x] A valid commit record is required for replay under this model.
[ ] The absence of a commit record forces recovery to apply exactly half the logged changes.
---
「不重放」對：本模型只重做已提交的交易。「保留舊狀態」對：題設尚未修改真正位置，忽略未完成交易即可維持舊版本。「有日誌資料就提交」錯：還缺有效的 commit。「需有效提交紀錄」對。「一定套用一半」錯：沒有這種規則，反而會破壞整筆處理的設計。

@q st5-08 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 情境
An ordered metadata-journal model must persist newly allocated file-data blocks before durably committing metadata that makes those blocks visible. User data are written to their home locations rather than copied into this metadata journal. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The ordering can prevent a committed new mapping from exposing a not-yet-written data block.
[x] Committing a new mapping before its data become durable could expose old contents after a crash.
[ ] Metadata-only journaling means that user-data blocks are never written to storage.
[x] This ordering rule alone does not guarantee survival of every later uncommitted update.
[ ] The rule requires copying every user-data block into the metadata journal itself.
---
「避免新映射指未寫好資料」對：先確保資料已保存，再讓提交後的 metadata 公開它。「先提交可見舊內容」對：若資料尚未完成，恢復映射後可能讀到不符本次寫入的內容。「資料永遠不寫」錯：資料是寫到真正位置，不是完全不寫。「未提交更新仍可掉」對：這不是所有應用寫入立即持久的保證。「全部進 metadata 日誌」錯：題設明確只在該日誌記 metadata。

@q st5-09 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 比較
In a simplified recovery model, a full check scans two million metadata records at 100000 records/s. Journal recovery instead replays 500 committed updates at 1000 updates/s. Ignore setup, data reads, and all other costs. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The full scan takes twenty seconds.
[x] The stated journal replay takes half a second.
[x] Replay work in this model depends on the committed updates that must be replayed.
[ ] The full scan takes half a second under the stated record rate.
[ ] Journaling guarantees that every user write previously stored only in volatile memory survives the crash.
---
「二十秒」對：2000000÷100000＝20。「半秒」對：500÷1000＝0.5。「取決於需重放的更新」對：本題工作量依這些已提交項目計算。「全掃半秒」錯：混用了日誌與全掃的數量和速率。「所有易失寫入都保存」錯：日誌不會自動保存從未寫到永久儲存的內容。

@q st5-10 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 觀念
A simplified copy-on-write filesystem retains a complete old tree. It writes new data and metadata to unused blocks, makes those blocks durable, and then atomically publishes a durable new root pointer. The old root remains usable until publication. Assume the stated storage ordering is enforced. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] A crash before publication can leave the complete old tree as the selected version.
[x] A crash after successful publication can select the complete new tree.
[ ] Publishing the new root before its referenced blocks are durable is equally safe under every failure.
[x] The ordering of new-block persistence before root publication matters to the stated guarantee.
[ ] Every filesystem using copy-on-write must avoid every form of intent logging.
---
「先看到完整舊樹」對：根指標尚未改。「提交後可看到完整新樹」對：題設先持久化所有被參照的新塊，再發布根。「先改根也一樣安全」錯：根可能指到未保存完整的資料。「寫入順序重要」對：這是保證完整版本的條件。「COW 必完全不用意圖日誌」錯：COW 系統仍可用日誌處理同步寫入等需求；一致性與每次寫入的持久時點不能混為一談。

@q st5-11 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 找錯
A filesystem journals metadata to keep its on-disk structure consistent. New user-data bytes exist only in volatile memory when power is lost; those bytes have not reached any persistent data block or log. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The new bytes can be lost even if recovery restores a structurally consistent filesystem.
[x] Structural consistency and persistence of every recent user write are different guarantees.
[ ] Metadata journaling can recreate those never-persisted byte values from nothing.
[ ] A buffered write's return alone proves these bytes reached persistent storage.
[ ] A consistent recovered filesystem must contain the newest contents that existed only in volatile memory.
---
「資料可掉、結構仍一致」對：重建正確 metadata 不等於保存尚未寫出的內容。「兩種保證不同」對：一致性關心結構相容，持久性關心已要求保存的修改。「日誌憑空生資料」錯：題設任何永久位置都沒這些值。「buffered write 返回即永久」錯：本題明確仍在易失記憶體。「一致就一定最新」錯：完整舊狀態也可能一致。

@q st5-12 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 觀念
A metadata value initially equals 5. Recovery record Set stores the complete replacement value 6; recovery record Inc instead means increment the current value by one. Compare replaying each record twice from the initial state. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Replaying Set twice leaves the value equal to 6.
[x] Replaying Inc twice leaves the value equal to 7.
[x] A replay design must account for interruptions that cause the same committed change to be replayed again.
[ ] Replaying the raw increment twice has the same effect as replaying it once from value 5.
[ ] Storing a complete replacement value inherently makes two replays change 5 into 7.
---
「Set 兩次為 6」對：先設 6，再設 6，結果相同。「Inc 兩次為 7」對：5＋1＋1。「需考慮重做重複」對：恢復本身也可能中斷。「原始加一重做等於一次」錯：一次是 6，兩次是 7。「完整內容重放變 7」錯：題設保存指定值 6，不是把六當增加量；可重放的紀錄需有適當語意，不能只說所有操作天然安全。

@q st5-13 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 情境
On Linux, a program finishes writing a regular file and `fsync(fd)` succeeds. Assume no concurrent changes, no reported I/O error, and storage that honors completed flushes. Only this file descriptor is synchronized. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The call synchronizes the file's modified data and associated metadata to storage.
[x] The call does not close the file descriptor.
[ ] Synchronizing the file necessarily also persists a newly created name in its parent directory.
[x] Persistence of a new directory entry can require synchronizing the parent directory separately.
[ ] This single call automatically makes a transaction modifying several unrelated files all-or-nothing.
---
「檔案資料與相關 metadata」對：成功的 fsync 針對此檔案完成同步，並依題設相信儲存完成回報。「不關閉」對：它不是 close。「新名字必一併持久」錯：父目錄項不保證因此同步。「目錄需另同步」對：新建名稱的持久性涉及父目錄。「多檔交易自動原子」錯：同步一個檔案不會憑空提供跨檔交易機制。

@q st5-14 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 追蹤
A trusted recovery scan finds that one live inode references block 42, but the free-space bitmap marks that block free. No other inode claims it, and the scan confirms the block's data and inode reference are correct. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The bitmap should mark block 42 as allocated.
[x] Leaving it marked free risks assigning the same block to another file.
[ ] The mismatch proves that the valid data in block 42 must be erased.
[x] Correcting this one allocation-status bit can resolve the stated inconsistency without changing file data.
[ ] Marking the block free again preserves the trusted inode's exclusive ownership.
---
「標成已配置」對：題設已確認 inode 的使用資訊可信。「可能重複配置」對：配置器若相信空閒位元，可能再把 42 給別人。「必須抹掉資料」錯：本題資料與指標本來正確。「改一位可解決」對：矛盾在唯一的使用狀態位元。「再標空閒可保獨占」錯：那反而留下重複配置風險。

@q st5-15 | os-storage#5 | 2 | 計算
完整日誌（資料和 metadata 都先寫進日誌）時，每份資料要寫到磁碟幾次？
== 2
---
一次進日誌、一次到原位置。所以 full journaling 寫入量約加倍，換取最高的一致性。

@q st5-16 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼「先寫日誌、commit 之後才改原位置」能保證當機後檔案系統一致。
---
當機時有三種情況：(1) commit 前：原位置完全沒動，丟掉日誌就是舊的一致狀態；(2) commit 後、checkpoint 中：日誌裡有這筆交易完整的新內容，重放一次就到新的一致狀態（重放是冪等的）；(3) checkpoint 完成：已經是新狀態。任何時間點恢復後都是「完整的舊」或「完整的新」，不會停在一半。

@q sy2-17 | os-sync#2 | 2 | 追蹤
麵包店演算法（n 個行程）：號碼 0 表示不想進。目前 P0～P4 的號碼是 0、3、3、0、2。依序是誰進入臨界區？
== 4,1,2
---
比較 (號碼, 編號)，小的先：P4 (2)、P1 (3,1)、P2 (3,2)。號碼相同時用編號打破平手。

@q sy2-18 | os-sync#2 | 2 | 陷阱
Two processes use a single shared variable `turn` to take turns strictly. Process $P_i$ (with $j = 1 - i$) runs:
`while (turn != i) ;  /* critical section */  turn = j;`
Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The two processes can never be in their critical sections at the same time.
[x] If $P_1$ never wants to enter its critical section, then after $P_0$ leaves once, $P_0$ cannot enter again, which violates the progress requirement.
[ ] Since $P_1$ is not interested, $P_0$ can enter again immediately.
[x] If both processes arrive together, only the process named by `turn` gets in.
[x] Peterson's solution adds the `flag` array so that a process can enter when the other process is not interested.
---
「互斥成立」對：`turn` 同時只能是一個值，只有 $P_{\text{turn}}$ 能通過 while。

「$P_1$ 不想進時 $P_0$ 進不了第二次」對：$P_0$ 離開時把 `turn` 設成 1，要等 $P_1$ 進出一次才會改回 0。沒有人在臨界區，$P_0$ 卻被一個不想進的行程擋住，違反 progress。

「$P_0$ 可以馬上再進」錯：`turn` 仍是 1。

「同時到達只有 `turn` 指定的進去」對：另一個卡在 while。

「Peterson 加 `flag`」對：看到對方的 flag 是 false，就不用等 `turn`。

@q sy2-19 | os-sync#2 | 3 | 找錯
學生寫 Peterson：`flag[i]=true; turn=j; while (flag[j] && turn==i);`。哪裡錯？
[x] 條件應是 `turn==j`。例如 P0 先進入臨界區，P1 接著設 turn=0，檢查 `turn==1` 不成立就直接進入，兩個同時在裡面
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 應該先設 turn 再設 flag
[ ] flag 應該設 false
---
正確版本中，後設定 turn 的一方會讓給對方；寫錯的版本變成「後到的人看到 turn 不是自己就進去」，剛好跟原意相反，互斥被破壞。

@q sy2-20 | os-sync#2 | 2 | 比較
Compare Lamport's bakery algorithm with Peterson's solution for mutual exclusion. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The bakery algorithm handles $n$ processes, while Peterson's solution as usually presented handles only two.
[ ] In the bakery algorithm, ticket numbers never exceed the number of processes.
[ ] Both algorithms require a special hardware instruction that reads and writes a memory word in one indivisible step.
[x] In the bakery algorithm, two processes may take the same ticket number, and the tie is broken by process number.
[ ] Peterson's solution works for $n$ processes simply by letting `turn` range over $n$ values.
---
「麵包店可處理 $n$ 個」對：每個人拿號碼牌，號碼小的先進。

「號碼不超過行程數」錯：只要一直有人在臨界區或等待，新號碼就是目前最大值加 1，永遠不會回到 0，可以一直變大。

「都需要特殊硬體指令」錯：兩者都只用一般的讀寫，這是它們的重點（但在會重排記憶體存取的現代 CPU 上，需要額外的順序保證）。

「同號時用行程編號決定」對：拿號碼不是不可分割的，兩人可能拿到同一號，所以比較 (號碼, 編號) 這一對。

「`turn` 改成 $n$ 個值就能推廣」錯：直接這樣改無法保證互斥；要推廣到 $n$ 個行程需要分好幾層過濾（filter 演算法）之類更複雜的設計。

@q sy2-21 | os-sync#2 | 3 | 證明
證明 Peterson 演算法滿足「進行」：沒有人在臨界區、有人想進時，一定有人能進去。
---
P$i$ 只會卡在 `while (flag[j] && turn==j)`。若 P$j$ 不想進，flag[j] 為 false，P$i$ 直接進。若兩個都想進，兩人都卡住需要 turn==j 且 turn==i 同時成立，不可能；turn 只有一個值，其中一人的條件不成立，那人就能進入。

@q sy5-16 | os-sync#5 | 2 | 觀念
Inside a monitor, process $P$ calls `x.signal()` while process $Q$ is waiting on condition `x`. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] Under Hoare semantics (signal and wait), $P$ keeps running and $Q$ waits until $P$ leaves the monitor.
[ ] Under Hoare semantics, $Q$ must still recheck its condition in a `while` loop, since another process may have changed it.
[ ] Under Mesa semantics (signal and continue), $Q$ also runs immediately and $P$ waits.
[x] Under Mesa semantics, $Q$ must recheck its condition in a `while` loop after it wakes up.
[ ] Under either semantics, $P$ and $Q$ may run inside the monitor at the same time.
---
「Hoare：$P$ 繼續跑、$Q$ 等」錯：那是 Mesa。Hoare 是 $P$ 暫停、$Q$ 馬上跑，$P$ 進到專門的等候區，之後比外面的人先回來。

「Hoare 下仍要 `while`」錯：$Q$ 馬上接手，中間沒有別人能插進來改變狀態，條件仍成立，用 `if` 即可。

「Mesa 也是 $Q$ 馬上跑」錯：Mesa 是 $P$ 繼續跑，$Q$ 只是被移到就緒，等 $P$ 離開後才有機會。

「Mesa 要用 `while` 重新檢查」對：$Q$ 真正跑起來之前，別人可能已經把條件改掉。實務上（例如 Java、pthread）都是 Mesa，所以等待一律寫 `while`。

「兩人同時在監視器裡」錯：不管哪種語意，監視器裡同時只能有一個在執行。

@q sy5-17 | os-sync#5 | 2 | 陷阱
用監視器寫的哲學家用餐：只有左右兩人都沒在吃才能拿筷子。「不會大家互相卡死，所以也不會有人一直吃不到。」對嗎？
[ ] 對
[x] 不對
---
左右兩邊的人可能輪流吃，中間那位一直等不到兩邊同時空出來，就是飢餓。沒有死結不代表沒有飢餓。

@q sy5-18 | os-sync#5 | 3 | 找錯
學生用 pthread：`if (!ready) pthread_cond_wait(&cv, &m);`，但呼叫前沒有先鎖住 m。會出什麼問題？
[x] 檢查 ready 和開始等待之間，另一條執行緒可能設好 ready 並 signal，這個 signal 沒人收到，之後就永遠睡著；而且 wait 規定呼叫時要持有 m
[ ] 沒有問題
[ ] 會變成忙碌等待
[ ] 會同時醒來兩次
---
正確寫法：先鎖 m，用 `while (!ready)` 包住 wait，wait 會原子地放開 m 並睡覺。

@q sy5-19 | os-sync#5 | 3 | 觀念
A monitor is implemented with semaphores: a semaphore `mutex` (initially 1) guards entry to the monitor, and each condition variable `x` gets a semaphore `x_sem` and an integer `x_count`. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] `x_sem` is initialized to 0, so a process calling `x.wait()` sleeps on it.
[x] `x.signal()` calls `signal(x_sem)` only when `x_count > 0`, so a signal with no waiting process has no effect.
[x] If `x_sem` started at 1, the first `x.wait()` would wrongly pass without sleeping.
[x] Every monitor procedure begins with `wait(mutex)` so that at most one process runs inside the monitor.
[ ] `x.wait()` may call `wait(x_sem)` while still holding `mutex`, because the signaling process does not need `mutex`.
---
「`x_sem` 初值 0」對：條件變數的 wait 一定要睡，所以號誌從 0 開始。

「有人等才 signal」對：用 `x_count` 記有幾個人在等；沒人等時什麼都不做，符合條件變數「不記憶」的語意。如果直接對號誌 signal，號誌會把它記住，下一個 wait 就不睡了。

「初值 1 時第一個 wait 會錯誤通過」對：違反語意。

「每個程序先 `wait(mutex)`」對：這就是監視器的自動互斥。

「睡著時還拿著 `mutex`」錯：別人永遠進不了監視器，也就沒人能 signal 它，死結。wait 前要先放掉 `mutex`（或交給等候區的人）。

@q sy5-20 | os-sync#5 | 2 | 比較
實務上（Java、pthread）多用 Mesa 語意而不是 Hoare 語意。原因？
[x] Mesa 讓 signal 的人繼續跑，實作簡單、少一次切換；代價只是醒來的人要用 while 再檢查一次條件
[ ] Mesa 保證條件一定成立
[ ] Hoare 不能實作
[ ] Mesa 不需要鎖
---
多檢查一次條件也順便處理了「無故被叫醒」的情況。

@q dl4-17 | os-deadlock#4 | 3 | 計算
等待圖有兩個環：P1→P2→P1 和 P2→P3→P4→P2。終止成本 P1＝5、P2＝8、P3＝2、P4＝3。要打斷所有環，最小總成本？
== 7
---
只殺 P2 成本 8；殺 P1 打斷第一個環，再殺 P3（2）打斷第二個，合計 7。殺掉共同的點不一定最便宜。

@q dl4-18 | os-deadlock#4 | 2 | 情境
A system resolves deadlock with checkpoints and rollback. Which of the following statements about taking checkpoints more often are correct?
[x] Less work is lost on a rollback.
[x] More time and space are spent on checkpoints during normal operation.
[ ] Taking checkpoints more often makes rollback unnecessary altogether.
[ ] It reduces both the normal overhead and the amount of work lost on a rollback.
[ ] The frequency affects only normal overhead, not how much work a rollback loses.
---
checkpoint 越頻繁，rollback 退回的距離越短，「損失的工作越少」；但平時「要花更多時間和空間」存 checkpoint。這是平時開銷和出事損失之間的取捨，不可能兩邊都降，也不會讓 rollback 變得不需要。

@q dl4-19 | os-deadlock#4 | 1 | 計算
行程每 10 分鐘存一次 checkpoint。第 37 分鐘發生死結，它被 rollback 到最近的 checkpoint。損失幾分鐘的工作？
== 7
---
最近的 checkpoint 在第 30 分鐘。若沒有 checkpoint，就得整個重來，損失 37 分鐘。

@q dl4-20 | os-deadlock#4 | 3 | 陷阱
With several instances per resource, several deadlocked processes are detected and one of them is terminated. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The others cannot necessarily continue, since the released resources may not suffice.
[x] Detection should be run again, terminating another process if needed.
[x] Terminating one at a time costs more detection runs but loses less work.
[ ] Once one deadlocked process is terminated, all the others can always continue at once.
[ ] Every process in the deadlock must be terminated together, or the deadlock remains.
---
釋放的資源「可能不夠」其他人用，所以要「重新偵測」，必要時再終止下一個；「一次終止一個」偵測次數多但損失少。終止一個不保證全部解開，也不必一次全部終止。

@q me5-17 | os-memory#5 | 2 | 計算
段表：第 0 段 base 2000、limit 600；第 1 段 base 5200、limit 300；第 2 段 base 800、limit 1000。邏輯位址 (2, 999) 和 (1, 300) 的實體位址？
== 1799,越界
?? (2;999),(1;300)
---
$800+999=1799$（999 < 1000）。第 1 段長度 300，合法位移是 0～299，300 會觸發錯誤。

@q me5-18 | os-memory#5 | 2 | 計算
32 位元邏輯位址，每段最大 64 KB。最多可以有幾段？
== 65536
---
位移 16 位元（$2^{16}=64$ K），剩下 16 位元當段號：$2^{16}$ 段。

@q me5-19 | os-memory#5 | 2 | 情境
程式碼段被設成「可讀、可執行、不可寫」。程式有 bug 想寫入程式碼段，會怎樣？
[x] 硬體檢查段表的保護位元，發現違規，觸發錯誤（trap），作業系統通常終止程式
[ ] 寫入成功
[ ] 寫到資料段
[ ] 被忽略
---
每段有自己的權限，這是分段的優點之一：段跟程式的邏輯單位一致。

@q me5-20 | os-memory#5 | 3 | 陷阱
段表長度暫存器（STLR）是 4。程式用了段號 4 的位址。會怎樣？
[x] 段號必須小於 STLR，4 不合法，直接觸發錯誤（trap），不會去查段表
[ ] 用第 4 段
[ ] 用第 0 段
[ ] 自動配一個新段
---
段號從 0 開始，STLR＝4 表示只有 0～3 四段。先檢查段號、再檢查位移是否小於 limit。

@q st5-17 | os-storage#5 | 2 | 計算
完整日誌模式（資料和 metadata 都先寫進日誌）寫一個 100 MB 的新檔。資料部分大約總共要寫多少 MB 到磁碟？
== 200
---
先寫進日誌 100 MB，commit 後再寫到真正位置 100 MB。所以多數系統預設只記 metadata。

@q st5-18 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 陷阱
A new one-block append would require data in block 42, a new inode mapping, and an allocation-bitmap update. After a crash, only the new data bytes reached block 42. The durable inode still has length zero and no mapping to 42, and the durable bitmap still marks 42 free. Assume that was the consistent old state and no other reference exists. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The recovered inode can still describe the old empty file.
[x] The new bytes alone do not make block 42 part of the file.
[ ] Writing data bytes alone automatically changes the inode's durable length.
[x] The old metadata can remain mutually consistent even though the attempted append is lost.
[ ] Any nonzero bytes inside a block marked free necessarily prove structural inconsistency.
---
「仍是空檔」對：永久 inode 的長度與映射都沒變。「新內容不自動入檔」對：還需要正確 metadata 連結。「資料自己改 inode 長度」錯：這是不同位置的更新。「一致但寫入遺失」對：兩份 metadata 都保留完整舊狀態。「空閒塊非零必毀損」錯：空閒表示未分配，並不要求內容全是零。

@q st5-19 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 追蹤
同樣的更新，當機時 inode 和 bitmap 都寫好了，**資料區塊**還沒寫。結果？
[x] metadata 一致，但 inode 指向的區塊內容是舊的垃圾，使用者會讀到錯的資料
[ ] 一切正常
[ ] bitmap 和 inode 矛盾
[ ] 檔案消失
---
ordered 模式要求先寫資料、再 commit metadata，就是為了避免這種情況。

@q st5-20 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 比較
soft updates 和日誌，在當機一致性上的做法差在哪？
[x] soft updates 安排 metadata 寫入的先後順序，讓磁碟上任何時候最多只有「可回收的空間浪費」；日誌則是先把修改寫到別處，當機後重放
[ ] 兩者一樣
[ ] soft updates 需要日誌
[ ] 日誌不需要額外空間
---
soft updates 不用額外寫日誌，但實作複雜得多。

@q sy6-18 | os-sync#6 | 2 | 計算
低優先權 L 拿著鎖、還要 5 ms 才放；高優先權 H 等這把鎖；中優先權 M 有 30 ms 的工作，會搶占 L。H 要等多久？用優先權繼承呢？
== 35,5
?? 沒有繼承,有繼承
---
沒有繼承：M 先跑 30 ms，L 再跑 5 ms，H 等 35 ms。有繼承：L 暫時升到 H 的優先權，M 搶不走，H 只等 5 ms。

@q sy6-19 | os-sync#6 | 2 | 情境
Two processes each try to get two locks; when a process cannot get its second lock, it releases its first lock and retries. In practice they keep releasing and retrying at the same moments, and neither makes progress. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Waiting a random amount of time before each retry breaks the symmetry, so one process soon gets both locks.
[x] If both processes always wait the same fixed amount of time before retrying, they can keep colliding forever.
[ ] Replacing the waiting with busy waiting solves the problem.
[ ] Ethernet avoids repeated collisions by making every sender wait the same fixed time after a collision.
[ ] In this situation both processes are blocked in the waiting state.
---
這是活鎖：兩邊都很忙，卻都沒有進展。

「隨機等待打破同步」對：兩人等的時間不同，先醒來的那個就能一次拿到兩把鎖。

「固定等一樣久會一直撞」對：兩人的節奏完全一樣，下一次又同時拿、同時放。

「改成忙碌等待」錯：忙碌等待只是換一種等法，節奏一樣時仍然同步。

「乙太網路等固定時間」錯：固定時間會讓碰撞的兩方再撞一次；乙太網路是各自等**隨機**時間再送，道理和隨機等待相同。

「兩人都在 waiting 狀態」錯：活鎖的行程一直在跑（拿鎖、放鎖），這正是它跟死結不同的地方。

@q sy6-20 | os-sync#6 | 2 | 找錯
A student says: "Aging, which raises a process's priority the longer it waits, solves deadlock." Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Aging addresses starvation, not deadlock.
[x] In a deadlock, each process waits for a resource held by another process in the cycle, so raising priorities does not release any resource.
[x] Even the highest-priority process in a deadlock cycle cannot obtain the resource it waits for.
[x] A deadlock can be broken by taking a resource away from some process or by terminating some process in the cycle.
[x] In starvation the system still makes progress, while in a deadlock the processes in the cycle make none.
---
「aging 處理飢餓」對：飢餓是「一直有人插隊、輪不到」，提高久等者的優先權就輪得到。

「死結中互等資源，提高優先權沒用」對、「優先權最高也拿不到」對：每個人都卡在等別人手上的資源，排程器讓它跑，它也只是繼續等。

「搶走資源或終止某個行程」對：這是死結恢復的兩種方法，打破循環等待。

「飢餓有進展、死結沒有」對：飢餓時只是某個人一直輪不到；死結時循環裡的人全部動不了。五句都對。

@q dl2-19 | os-deadlock#2 | 3 | 計算
R1 有 2 個實例、R2 有 1 個，現在都已分配完。P1 持有 1 個 R1、要 R2；P2 持有 R2、要 1 個 R1；P3 持有 1 個 R1、要 R2。有幾個行程處於死結？
== 3
---
可用 (0,0)：P1、P3 等 R2（在 P2 手上），P2 等 R1（在 P1、P3 手上），沒有任何人能先完成，三個都死結。

@q dl2-20 | os-deadlock#2 | 2 | 計算
資源配置圖（每種資源 1 個實例）：P1→R1→P2、P2→R2→P3、P3→R3→P1（行程→資源表示請求、資源→行程表示持有）。轉成等待圖有幾條邊？有死結嗎？
== 3,有
?? 邊,死結
---
把資源點拿掉：P1→P2、P2→P3、P3→P1，形成環；單一實例時有環就是死結。

@q me1-18 | os-memory#1 | 3 | 情境
A loader may place the same program image at different bases. Inside the image, an instruction located 100 bytes from the start of the image calls a routine located 300 bytes from the start. The instruction can encode its target either as an absolute address or as a signed distance from its own address:
```
image + 100:  call  target     ; routine at image + 300
```
The image is loaded once at base 4096 and, in another run, at base 12288, and the loader does not modify any instruction bytes; the routine itself is never moved relative to the instruction. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The relative distance to the routine is 200 bytes at either base.
[ ] The target addresses at the two bases are 4396 and 12388.
[ ] An unchanged absolute target of 4396 selects the intended routine at both bases without relocation.
[x] Relative references can help identical code bytes work at different image locations.
[ ] Moving the image by 8192 bytes requires changing this internal relative distance from 200 to 8392.
---
「距離都 200」對：300−100＝200，整份影像平移不改內部差距。「4396 與 12388」錯：12288＋300＝12588。「固定 4396 兩處都對」錯：第二份的目標已在 12588。「相對參照可支援同碼不同位置」對。「距離加 8192」錯：指令與目標一起移動，兩者差距不變。

@q me1-19 | os-memory#1 | 3 | 找錯
A program was compiled to run at physical base 0x4000, and one instruction directly contains the absolute address 0x4200 of a lookup table that sits 0x200 bytes into the image:
```
0x4010:  load  r2, [0x4200]   ; lookup table
```
To make room for another program, the whole image is copied unchanged to a region that starts 0x4000 bytes higher, at 0x8000. The system rewrites no addresses at load time and has no execution-time translation, and nothing else in memory changes during the move. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The intended relocated data would be at 0x8200.
[x] The unchanged instruction still refers to 0x4200.
[ ] Merely loading the image at its new location automatically changes the instruction's stored address to 0x8200.
[ ] No relocation mechanism is needed, because the data moved together with the code.
[ ] A limit check alone adds 0x4000 to the stored address.
---
「資料在 0x8200」對：0x4200＋0x4000＝0x8200。「指令仍 0x4200」對：題設沒有改寫或轉換。「搬檔自行改地址」錯：搬動位元組不會自動修補其中的絕對地址。「資料跟著搬所以不需重定位」錯：資料搬了、指令裡的位址沒改，就會存取到舊位置；需要重定位機制或重新產生絕對碼。「limit 加起點」錯：limit 做範圍保護，不負責地址加法。

@q me1-20 | os-memory#1 | 3 | 陷阱
「行程的邏輯位址空間不可能比實體記憶體大。」對嗎？
[ ] 對
[x] 不對
---
有了虛擬記憶體，邏輯位址空間只受位址位元數限制（例如 64 位元），只有用到的部分才需要放在實體記憶體裡。

@q st4-18 | os-storage#4 | 2 | 計算
bitmap 中 0 表示空閒、1 表示已用，從第 0 塊開始是 `1111 1111 1110 1100`。第一個空閒的區塊是第幾塊？
== 11
---
前 11 個位元（第 0～10 塊）都是 1，第 11 個位元是 0。CPU 可以一次看一個字是否全 1，很快跳過已滿的部分。

@q st4-19 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 陷阱
A directory contains a symbolic link named `again` that resolves back to the same directory. A pathname resolver permits at most eight symbolic-link traversals for one lookup. Consider a path requiring nine uses of `again`. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The path would exceed the stated traversal limit.
[x] Links to directories can produce repeated traversal of the same directory object.
[ ] Creating the symbolic link requires copying the complete target directory contents.
[x] A traversal limit can prevent this lookup from following links indefinitely.
[ ] The nine-link path must succeed because eight traversals are permitted.
---
「超過限制」對：九次大於最多八次。「可能重複走相同目錄」對：這條連結每次都回到自己。「複製內容」錯：符號連結保存的是路徑參照。「限制可避免無止境追蹤」對。「九次必通過」錯：允許八次不等於允許第九次；此處八次是題設模型，不拿來代表特定作業系統的固定數值。

@q st4-20 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 比較
A free-block linked list visits physical blocks in order 50,200,51,201,52. Each block stores only the next address, with no auxiliary address index. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Blocks 50,51,52 are physically consecutive despite being separated in list order.
[x] List adjacency does not necessarily mean physical adjacency.
[x] Reaching the fifth listed block from the known first address requires following four next pointers.
[ ] The next pointer in block 50 must designate block 51 because 51 is the next physical block.
[ ] Having five free blocks guarantees a run of five consecutive free blocks in this example.
---
「50、51、52 連續」對：它們的編號相鄰，但鏈結穿插其他地址。「鏈結鄰近不等於實體鄰近」對。「四次跳轉」對：五個位置之間有四個鏈結。「50 必指 51」錯：題設明定它指 200。「五塊保證連續五塊」錯：最長連續段是三塊，總空閒數量不代表連續長度。

@q pr3-20 | os-process#3 | 2 | 計算
某個舊系統上，行程的使用者位址空間只有 3 GB，每條執行緒的堆疊預設保留 8 MB 的位址範圍。不考慮其他用途，最多大約能開幾條執行緒？
== 384
---
$3\times1024/8=384$。實際上程式碼、heap 也要空間，會更少；這是 32 位元時代大量執行緒伺服器常碰到的上限，改小堆疊或改用 64 位元就能解決。

@q pr3-21 | os-process#3 | 3 | 陷阱
有 4 條執行緒的行程，其中一條呼叫 `fork()`（POSIX）。子行程裡有幾條執行緒？
== 1
---
只有呼叫 fork 的那條會被複製。若其他執行緒當時拿著鎖，子行程裡那把鎖永遠不會被放開，所以 fork 後通常馬上 exec。

@q pr3-22 | os-process#3 | 2 | 計算
多對多模型：10 條使用者執行緒由 3 條核心執行緒輪流承載，機器有 8 核。同一時間最多幾條使用者執行緒真的在 CPU 上跑？
== 3
---
真正被排到 CPU 上的是核心執行緒，只有 3 條。核心再多也用不到。

@q pr4-20 | os-process#4 | 2 | 陷阱
A program in user mode executes a privileged instruction that disables interrupts. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The CPU refuses to execute it in user mode and raises a trap to the operating system.
[ ] Interrupts are disabled.
[ ] The instruction is skipped as if it did nothing, and the program continues.
[x] The operating system usually terminates the program.
[x] If user programs could disable interrupts, a program could keep the CPU forever, because the timer interrupt could no longer take it away.
---
「CPU 拒絕、trap 給 OS」對：模式位元是使用者模式，特權指令不能執行。

「中斷被關閉」錯。

「當成沒事跳過」錯：會觸發例外，不會默默略過。

「通常終止程式」對。

「能關中斷就能霸佔 CPU」對：這正是要把它設成特權指令的原因。

@q pr4-21 | os-process#4 | 1 | 觀念
硬體怎麼知道目前是核心模式還是使用者模式？
[x] 處理器有一個記錄目前模式的硬體旗標，進入核心（中斷、trap、系統呼叫）時硬體自動切成核心模式，返回時切回
[ ] 看程式的檔名
[ ] 看記憶體位址
[ ] 作業系統每條指令都檢查一次
---
切換一定要透過硬體定義好的入口，使用者程式不能自己把旗標改成核心模式。

@q pr4-22 | os-process#4 | 3 | 計算
磁碟以 400 MB/s 傳資料。每 4 KB 一次中斷、每次處理 5 µs 時，CPU 有幾 % 花在中斷？改用 DMA、每 1 MB 才中斷一次呢？
== 50%,0.2%
== 0.5,0.002
?? 每4KB,每1MB
---
400 MB/s ÷ 4 KB ≈ $10^5$ 次/秒 × 5 µs ＝ 0.5 秒/秒。DMA 時 400 次/秒 × 5 µs ＝ 2 ms/秒。

@q sc1-20 | os-sched#1 | 1 | 計算
40 ms 內完成了 5 個工作。吞吐量是每秒幾個工作？
== 125
---
$5/0.04$。吞吐量看單位時間完成多少，跟個別工作等多久無關。

@q sc1-21 | os-sched#1 | 3 | 陷阱
Running the shortest available job first minimizes the average waiting time. In one system, a long job $L$ needing 50 ms arrives at time 0, and from time 0 on a new short job needing 2 ms arrives every 2 ms, forever. The scheduler always picks the shortest job that is waiting whenever the CPU becomes free. Which of the following statements about the longest waiting time are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] In this system, $L$ waits forever, so shortest-first does not minimize the longest waiting time.
[ ] Shortest-first also minimizes the longest waiting time, since every job waits less.
[ ] The longest waiting time does not depend on the scheduling algorithm at all.
[ ] $L$ waits at most as long as the total of the short jobs that arrived before it, which is 0 ms here.
[ ] Minimizing the average waiting time always minimizes the worst case as well.
---
「L 永遠在等」對：每次 CPU 空下來，都剛好有一個新的 2 ms 工作在等，比 $L$ 短，就先做它；$L$ 一直被插隊，發生飢餓。照到達順序做，$L$ 等 0 就開始了。

「也讓最長等待最小」錯：見上。

「最長等待跟排程法無關」錯：這個例子換一種排法差別是無限大和 0。

「最多等前面短工作的總和」錯：後來的短工作也能插隊，等待沒有那樣的上限。

「平均最好就是最壞也最好」錯：不同的評估標準常常互相拉扯。

@q sc1-22 | os-sched#1 | 3 | 計算
每個行程有 80% 的時間在等 I/O。假設各行程獨立，同時有 4 個行程時 CPU 使用率大約多少？
== 59%
== 0.59
---
CPU 閒置要 4 個都在等：$0.8^4\approx0.41$，使用率 $1-0.41$。這說明多工（多放幾個行程在記憶體）能大幅提高 CPU 使用率。

@q sc3-20 | os-sched#3 | 2 | 計算
Rate-monotonic 的充分條件 $U\le n(2^{1/n}-1)$。$n=4$ 時上限大約是？
== 0.757
== 0.76
---
$4(2^{0.25}-1)\approx4\times0.1892$。$n$ 越大越接近 $\ln2\approx0.693$。

@q sc3-21 | os-sched#3 | 3 | 陷阱
Three periodic tasks have the parameters below, with deadlines equal to periods, and are released together at time 0:

| task | period | execution time |
|---|---|---|
| $T_1$ | 5 | 2 |
| $T_2$ | 10 | 3 |
| $T_3$ | 20 | 4 |

Their total utilization is $U = 0.4 + 0.3 + 0.2 = 0.9$, above the rate-monotonic (RM) bound of about 0.78 for 3 tasks. A student concludes that RM must miss a deadline, but a classmate notices that every period divides the next one and simulates RM over one hyperperiod of 20 time units instead. The student also wonders whether only EDF can handle task sets above the bound, and what the bound really guarantees. The classmate's timeline shows $T_3$ finishing its first job at time 18, two units before its deadline at time 20, and the student wants to check that timeline unit by unit. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] RM is not necessarily unable to schedule them, because the bound is only a sufficient condition.
[x] The periods are multiples of one another, and for such task sets RM can schedule any utilization up to 1.
[x] Simulating one hyperperiod (20 time units) under RM shows that no deadline is missed.
[ ] RM certainly misses some deadline, because $U$ exceeds the bound.
[ ] Only EDF can schedule a task set whose utilization exceeds the RM bound.
---
上限「只是充分條件」，超過時要模擬或做回應時間分析。週期 5、10、20「互為倍數」，RM 可以排到 $U=1$；「模擬一個超週期」：0–2、5–7、10–12、15–17 跑週期 5，2–5、12–15 跑週期 10，7–10、17–18 跑週期 20，全部在期限內（程式確認）。所以 RM 這裡可行，不必非用 EDF。

@q sc3-22 | os-sched#3 | 3 | 計算
RM：(週期 4, 執行 1)、(6, 2)、(12, 3)，三個在時間 0 同時釋放。第三個工作第一次完成在時間幾？
== 10
---
回應時間分析：$R=3+\lceil R/4\rceil\cdot1+\lceil R/6\rceil\cdot2$，從 3 開始迭代 6、7、9、10、10 收斂。10 ≤ 12，趕得上。

@q sc4-20 | os-sched#4 | 2 | 計算
CFS 中 nice 0 的權重是 1024。某行程權重 2048，實際跑了 10 ms，它的 vruntime 增加多少 ms？
== 5
---
vruntime 增加量 ＝ 實際時間 × 1024 ÷ 權重。權重大的 vruntime 長得慢，所以較常被挑中。

@q sc4-21 | os-sched#4 | 2 | 比較
Two cores have 5 and 0 ready jobs. Each moved job needs 2 ms to reload data into its destination's private cache, so migration is not free. In one trace a system timer initiates a move from the busy queue; in another, the idle core requests a job. Compare the initiators and their effects on queue imbalance, separating movement overhead from the useful execution that an otherwise idle core can perform. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] In push migration, the system periodically pushes work from busy processors to idle ones.
[x] In pull migration, an idle processor pulls work from a busy one.
[ ] Migration is free, since the moved task keeps its old processor's cache contents.
[ ] Pull migration makes the load more uneven, since idle processors take on extra work.
[ ] Push migration is used only on single-processor systems.
---
「push」系統定期把忙的推給閒的；「pull」閒下來的處理器主動拉。遷移會讓工作失去舊處理器 cache 裡的資料，不是免費的；pull 是讓閒的分擔，負載更平均；單處理器沒有地方可推。

@q sc4-22 | os-sched#4 | 2 | 找錯
Use a simplified classic CFS share model with target period 24 ms and task weights 1024,2048,1024. Ideal shares divide the period in proportion to weight. A 1-ms minimum granularity prevents arbitrarily short actual slices, and a scheduler may extend its period when that limit binds. Now add a fourth task of weight 1024, for which the minimum is still nonbinding. Compare these shares with a proposed fixed 10-ms slice for every task. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The model does not give every process a fixed equal slice.
[x] Ideal shares divide the target period in proportion to task weight.
[x] Minimum granularity prevents actual slices from shrinking without limit.
[x] With the period fixed and granularity nonbinding, adding a runnable task reduces each existing task share.
[ ] Every task receives exactly 10 ms regardless of its weight and the queue size.
---
「非固定同片」對。「按權重分配」對：起初總權重4096，分6、12、6毫秒。「有下限」對：真的碰到下限時可擴展週期。「未碰下限時新增會縮分享額」對：總權重5120後分4.8、9.6、4.8、4.8。「每人固定十」錯。

@q me2-20 | os-memory#2 | 3 | 比較
A contiguous allocator keeps its free holes in address order, with these sizes:

| hole | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| size (KiB) | 250 | 120 | 400 | 180 |

Requests arrive in the order 150, 100, 300, and 170 KiB. Each request is placed entirely inside one hole, the chosen hole is split, and its remainder stays at the same position as a smaller hole. When two holes are equally good, the earliest one in address order is used, and the simulation stops at the first request that cannot be placed. A student simulates first-fit, best-fit, and worst-fit, writing down the list of hole sizes after every request, to see which policies place all four requests. A classmate predicts that best-fit will fail first, because it leaves many tiny holes, and another predicts that worst-fit will do best, because it leaves the largest remainders. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] First-fit fails on the 170 KiB request.
[ ] Best-fit fails on the 300 KiB request.
[x] Worst-fit fails on the 300 KiB request.
[ ] Best-fit initially places the 150 KiB request in the 250 KiB hole.
[ ] First-fit leaves 50 KiB in the first hole after the first two requests.
---
「first-fit 在 170 失敗」錯：前兩筆用完 250，300 放 400，170 放 180，全過。「best-fit 在 300 失敗」錯：依序選 180、120、400、250，全過。「worst-fit 第三筆失敗」對：先把 400 切成 250，再把最早的 250 切成 150，此時最大只剩 250。「best-fit 先選 250」錯：最小夠大的洞是 180。「first-fit 剩 50」錯：250−150−100＝0。

@q me2-21 | os-memory#2 | 3 | 情境
A network server allocates its connection objects from a pool that reserves 4096 bytes divided into 64-byte slots. Each object occupies 48 bytes and must use one whole slot; metadata and any alignment beyond the slots can be ignored. At peak load, about 50 connections are live at the same time, and the developers want to know how many slots the pool has, how much of the reserved memory the live objects tie up, and what happens to a slot when a connection closes and a new one opens. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The pool contains 64 slots.
[x] Fifty live objects consume 3200 reserved slot bytes.
[ ] A pool containing fifty live objects has no unused bytes inside occupied slots.
[x] A released slot can be reused by another object of the same size.
[x] The pool holds at most 64 live objects, even though $4096/48$ exceeds 85.
---
