「64 格」對：4096÷64＝64。「占用 3200」對：50×64＝3200，其中真正物件資料為 2400。「格內無浪費」錯：每格有 16 位元組未用，50 格共 800。「可重用」對：相同大小的格子可直接再配置。「最多 64 個」對：本題每個一定占一格，不能按 48 位元組直接緊密堆放。

@q me2-22 | os-memory#2 | 3 | 陷阱
During compaction, the operating system moves a process image from base 1000 to base 5000 and updates the process's base register, so that the CPU's logical addresses now map to the new location. At the same moment, however, a disk controller is performing a transfer into the image: it was given the physical write address 1200 before the move, which targets the image's byte at relative position 200, and it writes directly to physical memory without going through the CPU's base register. The device's pending request is not changed by the move. A student must decide which physical address now holds that byte, where the device's write will land, and what the operating system should have done before moving the image. A classmate argues that the move is safe as soon as the base register has been updated, and another that pausing the CPU is enough. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The corresponding byte in the moved image is at physical address 5200.
[x] The device's old address 1200 no longer selects that moved byte.
[ ] Updating the CPU base register necessarily rewrites physical addresses already held by the device.
[ ] The move can be treated as safe as soon as the CPU base register has been updated.
[ ] Pausing only CPU instruction execution guarantees that the device cannot access the old address.
---
「5200」對：5000＋200。「1200 不再對應新位置」對：那是舊起點加 200。「改 CPU 就改裝置」錯：題設裝置自行保留實體地址。「CPU 更新完就安全」錯：裝置還拿著舊實體位址，會寫到舊位置；要先協調裝置，例如等待完成，或採合適的位址更新與固定策略。「只停 CPU 就夠」錯：裝置的既有請求可能仍在進行。

@q me4-20 | os-memory#4 | 2 | 計算
39 位元虛擬位址、頁 4 KB、每個頁表項目 8 bytes，每一層頁表剛好一頁大。需要幾層？
== 3
---
一頁 512 項，每層吃 9 位元。頁內位移 12 位元，剩 27 位元，$27/9=3$ 層（RISC-V 的 Sv39 就是這樣）。

@q me4-21 | os-memory#4 | 2 | 計算
4 層頁表，TLB 命中率 98%、查 TLB 時間忽略，記憶體存取 100 ns。平均每次存取多少 ns？
== 108
---
命中 100；沒中要讀 4 層頁表再讀資料，500。$0.98\times100+0.02\times500$。

@q me4-22 | os-memory#4 | 3 | 情境
32 位元、10/10/12 兩層頁表，每張表 4 KB。行程只用到兩頁，分別在位址空間的最開頭和最尾端。頁表總共佔多少 KB？
== 12
---
外層 1 張；兩頁在不同的 4 MB 區段，各需一張內層表，共 3 張。單層頁表要 $2^{20}\times4$ bytes ＝ 4 MB。

@q st2-20 | os-storage#2 | 1 | 計算
8 顆 4 TB 硬碟做 RAID 6，可用容量多少 TB？
== 24
---
兩顆的容量拿去放兩種同位資訊：$(8-2)\times4$。可以同時壞兩顆。

@q st2-21 | os-storage#2 | 2 | 計算
RAID 5 一條 stripe 有三個資料區塊 1011、0110、1100。同位區塊是什麼？若存 0110 的那顆壞了，怎麼還原？
== 0001
---
$1011\oplus0110\oplus1100=0001$。還原：其餘三塊 XOR，$1011\oplus1100\oplus0001=0110$。

@q st2-22 | os-storage#2 | 3 | 情境
12 顆 16 TB 硬碟做 RAID 5，壞一顆後重建要讀其他 11 顆全部的資料（約 176 TB），常花上一兩天。主要風險？
[x] 重建期間再壞一顆、或讀到無法修正的磁區錯誤，資料就救不回來；大容量陣列因此改用 RAID 6
[ ] 重建時速度太快
[ ] 重建不需要讀其他硬碟
[ ] 沒有風險
---
硬碟越大，重建越久、要讀的位元越多，出錯機率也跟著變大。

@q st3-19 | os-storage#3 | 3 | 計算
inode 有 12 個直接指標、1 個單間接、1 個雙間接；區塊 4 KB、每個指標 4 bytes。讀檔案第 5,000,000 個 byte，它落在哪一層？inode 已在記憶體時要讀幾個磁碟區塊？

?? 哪一層,讀幾塊

第 $\lfloor5000000/4096\rfloor=1220$ 塊。直接 0～11、單間接 12～1035，1220 落在雙間接：讀雙間接區塊、第二層間接區塊、資料區塊，共 3 次。

@q st3-20 | os-storage#3 | 2 | 計算
檔案用 extent（起點, 長度）記錄：(100, 50)、(400, 30)、(1000, 20)。檔案的第 60 塊（從 0 開始）在磁碟的第幾塊？

前 50 塊在第一段；第 60 塊是第二段的第 10 塊，$400+10$。

@q st3-21 | os-storage#3 | 3 | 情境
A video file occupies 1000 data blocks, stored as two physical runs of 500 consecutive blocks each, because the disk had no single free region large enough. One file-system design records the file with one 8-byte pointer per data block; another records it as extents, with one 16-byte descriptor per run that gives the starting block and the length. All other metadata can be ignored. The designers also consider what happens when a heavily fragmented disk forces the same 1000 blocks into many short runs, say 250 runs of 4 blocks. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Per-block pointers occupy 8000 bytes.
[x] Two extent descriptors occupy 32 bytes.
[ ] The two descriptors together occupy only 16 bytes.
[x] More fragmentation can require more extent descriptors for the same number of data blocks.
[ ] Using extents requires every file to occupy exactly one physical run.
---
「8000」對：1000×8。「32」對：兩段各一筆，2×16。「總共 16」錯：只算一段。「碎片多可增描述量」對：每段連續範圍需額外描述。「全檔必一段」錯：本題就用兩段，extent 是逐段描述，不要求整個檔案完全連續。

@q sy1-20 | os-sync#1 | 3 | 計算
`x` 初值 1。T1 做 `x = x * 2`、T2 做 `x = x + 1`，各自都是「讀、算、寫」三步，沒有加鎖。最後 x 可能是哪些值？
== 2,3,4
---
依序跑：先乘後加 3、先加後乘 4。兩邊都先讀到 1：T1 算出 2、T2 也算出 2，誰後寫都是 2。所以有三種可能。

@q sy1-21 | os-sync#1 | 3 | 情境
Two processes run the same code: first check that a file does not exist, then create the file and write to it. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] At most one of the two processes can find that the file does not exist, because the first check already reserves the name.
[x] Making check-and-create a single indivisible operation, such as opening with `O_CREAT | O_EXCL`, fixes it.
[ ] There is no problem, because each file operation by itself is indivisible.
[ ] This is not a race condition, since the two processes use different file descriptors.
[ ] Inserting a short sleep between the check and the creation fixes the problem.
---
「最多一個看到不存在」錯：檢查和建立之間有空隙，兩人可能都看到不存在、都建立，一個蓋掉另一個的資料。這種模式叫 check-then-act。

「合成單一不可分割的操作」對：`O_EXCL` 讓「不存在才建立」由核心一次做完，第二個人會失敗。

「每個呼叫本身不可分割所以沒問題」錯：問題在兩個呼叫**之間**，不在單一呼叫裡面。

「不同檔案描述子所以不是競爭條件」錯：它們爭的是同一個檔名，結果取決於兩人步驟的交錯，正是競爭條件。

「中間睡一下」錯：只是改變時機，空隙還在，甚至更大。

@q sy1-22 | os-sync#1 | 2 | 找錯
A shared structure has two fields `x` and `y` that must always be updated together. A student takes a lock only when writing them, not when reading, arguing that "reading does not change the data". Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] A reader may see the new `x` together with the old `y` if it runs in the middle of an update.
[x] As long as some thread may update the fields, the threads that read them also need to take the lock to see a consistent pair.
[ ] Reading never needs a lock, because it does not change the data.
[ ] If the structure is set before the threads start and never updated afterward, reading it still needs the lock.
[ ] Locking only the updates guarantees that every read sees a consistent pair.
---
「讀到新的 x 和舊的 y」對：寫入者拿著鎖改到一半，讀的一方根本不看鎖，直接讀到一半新一半舊的組合。

「有人寫時讀也要拿鎖」對：鎖只能排除同樣去拿鎖的人。

「讀永遠不用鎖」錯：只有在沒人寫的時候才成立。

「啟動前設好、之後不寫仍要鎖」錯：沒人寫就不可能讀到一半，不用鎖。

「只鎖寫入者就保證一致」錯：同第一句。

@q sy3-20 | os-sync#3 | 2 | 情境
Many threads only increment one shared counter. Compare protecting `count++` with a mutex against using an atomic `fetch_and_add` instruction. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] `fetch_and_add` increments in one hardware instruction, with no lock and no sleeping.
[ ] A mutex is always faster, because it is implemented in software and avoids special hardware instructions.
[x] When several variables must be changed together, a single `fetch_and_add` is not enough, and a lock is needed.
[x] Using a plain `count++` with neither protection is a race condition.
[x] Threads on different cores can apply `fetch_and_add` to the same counter at the same time.
---
「一條指令完成、不用鎖、不睡」對：硬體保證讀、加、寫不被打斷。

「mutex 一定比較快」錯：mutex 至少要一次原子操作來拿鎖、一次來放鎖，競爭時還可能讓執行緒睡著，成本更高。

「好幾個變數要一起改時要用鎖」對：一條原子指令只能保護一個位置。

「什麼都不用是競爭條件」對：讀、加、寫三步會交錯。

「不同核心可以同時用」對：原子指令正是為多核心設計的，硬體（透過快取一致性）保證它們依序生效。

@q sy3-21 | os-sync#3 | 2 | 找錯
`compare_and_swap(&x, expected, new)` sets `x` to `new` only if `x` equals `expected`, and reports whether it did so. A student increments a shared `x` with `old = x; compare_and_swap(&x, old, old + 1);` without a loop. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] If another thread changes `x` between the read and the CAS, the CAS still adds 1 to the new value.
[x] A correct version repeats the read and the CAS in a loop until the CAS succeeds.
[ ] The CAS always succeeds, because it is atomic.
[ ] The report of the CAS can be ignored, because a failed CAS retries automatically.
[ ] Even when only one thread ever modifies `x`, the student's version can lose increments.
---
「中間被改仍會加 1」錯：`x` 不等於 old，CAS 什麼都不做，學生又沒檢查結果，這次加 1 就不見了。

「用迴圈重讀、重試」對：`do { old = x; } while (!compare_and_swap(&x, old, old + 1));`。

「原子的所以一定成功」錯：原子保證的是「比較和寫入」不被打斷，不保證比較會相等。

「失敗會自動重試」錯：CAS 只做一次就回報結果，要不要重試由程式根據回報決定。

「只有一條執行緒在改也會漏」錯：沒有別人改，讀到的值到 CAS 時一定沒變，CAS 一定成功。

@q sy3-22 | os-sync#3 | 3 | 比較
test_and_set 和 compare_and_swap 的能力差異？
[x] test_and_set 只能把一個旗標設成 1，適合做簡單的鎖；compare_and_swap 能有條件地把任意值換掉，可以做出任意多條執行緒的無鎖資料結構
[ ] 兩者完全一樣
[ ] test_and_set 比較強
[ ] compare_and_swap 只能用在單核心
---
理論上 CAS 能解任意數量執行緒的共識問題，test_and_set 只能解兩條的。

@q sy4-20 | os-sync#4 | 3 | 陷阱
Threads $T_1$ and $T_2$ must meet at a point: neither may pass it until both have arrived. Two semaphores `arr1` and `arr2` start at 0. $T_1$ runs `wait(arr2); signal(arr1);` and $T_2$ runs `wait(arr1); signal(arr2);`. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Both threads wait forever, because each one waits for a signal that the other would send only after its own wait.
[x] A correct version lets each thread first signal its own arrival and then wait for the other: $T_1$ runs `signal(arr1); wait(arr2);` and $T_2$ runs `signal(arr2); wait(arr1);`.
[x] As written, neither thread ever passes the meeting point.
[x] In the correct version, neither thread passes the meeting point before the other one has arrived.
[x] A single semaphore that starts at 1, with each thread running `wait` and then `signal`, gives only mutual exclusion, not a meeting.
---
「兩邊永遠卡住」對、「誰都過不去」對：兩個號誌都是 0，兩人都先 wait，誰都不會先 signal，循環等待。

「先 signal 自己到了、再 wait 對方」對：誰先到都會先留下「我到了」的記號，再等對方的記號。

「正確版本中誰都不會先過」對：要過去必須 wait 到對方的 signal，而對方 signal 代表它已經到了。

「一個初值 1 的號誌只是互斥」對：先到的人直接 wait 通過，根本不用等對方。五句都對。

@q sy4-21 | os-sync#4 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「號誌的 signal 如果當時沒有人在等，就會不見。」錯在哪？
[x] 號誌有計數，signal 會把值加 1 記起來，之後的 wait 可以直接通過
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] signal 會讓值變 0
[ ] 號誌不能 signal
---
之後會學到的條件變數才是「沒人等就不見」，這是兩者最重要的差別之一。

@q sy4-22 | os-sync#4 | 3 | 證明
哲學家用餐：加一個初值 4 的號誌 room，每人先 `wait(room)` 才能坐下拿筷子，吃完 `signal(room)`。證明不會發生所有人都永遠卡住的情況。
---
最多 4 人同時在桌邊，但有 5 支筷子。由鴿籠原理，至少有一個人能拿到左右兩支（4 人分 5 支，必有一人拿 2 支），他吃完會放回筷子並離開，讓別人繼續。所以不可能所有在桌邊的人都永遠等待。

@q vm1-20 | os-vm#1 | 2 | 情境
A process extends its heap with `malloc` and its stack with a deep recursion, and each time it touches a page of the heap or the stack for the first time, a page fault occurs. These pages have never been written before and do not exist in the program's executable file. The machine has 8 GB of memory and is shared by many users, and the physical frame that the operating system picks for the new page may have belonged to another user's process a moment earlier. A student asks where the contents of such a page come from, for example for a 4 KiB stack page touched at depth 1000 of the recursion. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The operating system usually gives it a zero-filled frame instead of reading from disk.
[x] Zero-filling also keeps a process from seeing a previous user's data.
[ ] The page is read from the executable file, since every page of a program comes from there.
[ ] The page is read from swap space, where every new page is kept until first use.
[ ] The program is terminated, since the page was never loaded.
---
這些頁本來就沒有內容，「配一個清成全 0 的 frame」就好，不需要 I/O；清成 0 也是「安全需要」。執行檔只放程式碼和初始資料；還沒被換出過就不在 swap；這是合法存取，不會終止。

@q vm1-21 | os-vm#1 | 1 | 計算
純需求分頁，程式執行期間總共碰到 30 個不同的頁，系統給它 50 個 frame。會發生幾次 page fault？
== 30
---
每頁第一次碰到時缺頁一次；frame 夠多，不會有頁被換出再缺頁。

@q vm1-22 | os-vm#1 | 2 | 計算
記憶體存取 100 ns，每 $10^5$ 次存取有 1 次 page fault，處理一次 5 ms。有效存取時間？
== 150
---
$100+10^{-5}\times5\times10^6=100+50$ ns。缺頁率只要再高一點點，平均時間就會被缺頁主導。

@q vm3-19 | os-vm#3 | 2 | 計算
參考串 3 1 4 1 5 9 2 6 5 3 5，工作集視窗 $\Delta=5$。看完最後一個參考時，工作集大小？
== 4
---
最後 5 個參考是 2 6 5 3 5，不同的頁 {2, 3, 5, 6}。

@q vm3-20 | os-vm#3 | 3 | 陷阱
程式一直輪流用 3 頁：1 2 3 1 2 3 …（共 30 次）。只給 2 個 frame、用 LRU，和給 3 個 frame，各缺頁幾次？
== 30,3
?? 2個frame,3個frame
---
2 個 frame 時每次要的都剛好是剛被丟掉的那頁，30 次全缺頁；多一個 frame 就只剩第一輪的 3 次。frame 比工作集少一點點，效能就崩潰，這就是 thrashing 的縮影。

@q vm3-21 | os-vm#3 | 3 | 觀念
The page size is increased, for example from 4 KB to 64 KB. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The number of page faults usually decreases, because each fault brings in more data.
[x] The memory occupied by the working set may grow, because pages contain more unused parts.
[x] Page size is a trade-off among faults, page-table size and internal fragmentation.
[x] The page table becomes smaller, since there are fewer pages.
[ ] Both the number of page faults and the memory occupied by the working set always decrease together.
---
大頁一次帶進更多，「缺頁通常變少」，頁數少「頁表也變小」；但頁裡很多用不到的部分也佔著記憶體，「工作集的記憶體可能變大」。所以頁大小是「缺頁、頁表大小、內部碎片之間的取捨」，不是兩者一起變好。

@q vm4-19 | os-vm#4 | 2 | 情境
Two processes map the same file with `mmap` in shared mode, and one of them writes to the mapping. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] The other process sees the write.
[ ] Each process has its own copy, so the other process does not see the write.
[ ] The write becomes visible only after it has been written back to disk.
[ ] The write becomes visible only after the writer calls `munmap`.
[ ] With a private mapping, the other process would also see the write.
---
共享模式下兩者的頁表指向同一批實體 frame（頁快取裡的檔案頁），寫入直接反映，「另一個行程看得到」，不必等寫回磁碟或解除映射。私有模式（`MAP_PRIVATE`）用 copy-on-write，寫入只改自己的副本，別人看不到。

@q vm4-20 | os-vm#4 | 3 | 觀念
When a swapped-out process is brought back, $s$ of its pages are loaded in advance, and a fraction $\alpha$ of them will actually be used. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Loading in advance pays off when $\alpha$ is close to 1.
[x] It saves about $\alpha s$ page faults while loading about $(1-\alpha)s$ useless pages.
[x] This is why usually only pages from the process's working set are loaded in advance.
[ ] Loading in advance pays off when $\alpha$ is close to 0, since few pages are then wasted.
[ ] Loading in advance always pays off, since a page fault always costs more than a load.
---
「省下 $\alpha s$ 次缺頁、多載 $(1-\alpha)s$ 個沒用的頁」，$\alpha$「接近 1」時前者大於後者才划算；工作集裡的頁很可能馬上用到，所以「通常只預先載入工作集」。$\alpha$ 接近 0 時幾乎全白載；不是一定划算。

@q vm4-21 | os-vm#4 | 2 | 計算
資料大小隨機，平均最後一頁大約浪費半頁。頁 4 KB 和 64 KB 時，每個區段平均浪費幾 KB？
== 2,32
?? 4KB頁,64KB頁
---
半頁。頁越大，內部浪費越多；頁越小，頁表越大、缺頁越多。

@q dl1-21 | os-deadlock#1 | 2 | 情境
5 位哲學家都「先拿左邊的筷子、再拿右邊的」，可能卡死。只改其中一位，讓他先拿右邊再拿左邊。為什麼就不會卡死？
[x] 不可能再形成「每人都拿著左邊、等右邊」的環：那一位跟鄰居會搶同一支筷子，循環等待被打破
[ ] 因為他吃得比較快
[ ] 因為筷子變多了
[ ] 其實還是會卡死
---
這等於給筷子一個全域順序，大家都照順序拿；破壞的是「循環等待」條件。

@q dl1-22 | os-deadlock#1 | 2 | 計算
為了打破「持有並等待」，行程開始時就要申請所有會用到的資源。某行程跑 60 分鐘，只有最後 1 分鐘用到印表機，但從頭佔到尾。這台印表機的使用率最多多少（%）？
== 1.7
== 1.67
---
$1/60\approx1.67\%$，其餘 59 分鐘別人都不能用。這就是這種預防法的代價：資源利用率很低，還可能讓需要很多資源的行程一直等不到。

@q dl1-23 | os-deadlock#1 | 3 | 比較
Two threads use `trylock`: if one cannot get its second lock, it releases its first lock and retries immediately. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Deadlock cannot occur, since no thread holds a lock while waiting.
[x] Both threads may keep grabbing and releasing in step, making no progress.
[x] The threads stay busy the whole time, unlike deadlocked threads, which just sleep.
[x] Waiting a random time before retrying reduces this problem.
[ ] Exactly one of the two threads is guaranteed to finish first without ever needing to retry.
---
拿不到就放，不再「持有並等待」，「不會死結」。但兩邊可能一直同時拿、同時放，「空轉沒有進展」；這時執行緒「一直在忙」，和死結時睡著不動不同（這叫活鎖）；重試前「隨機等待」可以錯開。沒有任何保證哪一條一次就成功。

@q dl2-21 | os-deadlock#2 | 2 | 陷阱
「資源配置圖沒有環，就一定沒有死結（不管每種資源有幾個實例）。」對嗎？
[x] 對
[ ] 不對
---
死結必定有循環等待，所以一定有環。反過來不成立：有多個實例時，有環不一定死結。

@q dl2-22 | os-deadlock#2 | 2 | 情境
每種資源只有一個實例，系統用「宣告邊」做死結避免：P 申請 R 時，把宣告邊 P→R 改成配置邊 R→P。什麼情況下拒絕這個申請？
[x] 改了之後圖中（包含宣告邊）出現環，代表之後可能卡死
[ ] R 正在被別人用
[ ] P 已經持有其他資源
[ ] 永遠不拒絕
---
偵測環要 $O(n^2)$；資源有多個實例時就要改用 Banker。

@q dl3-22 | os-deadlock#3 | 2 | 計算
只有一種資源共 10 個。P0 最大需求 7、已配 3；P1 最大 4、已配 2；P2 最大 5、已配 2。系統安全嗎？一個安全順序？
== 安全,P1 P2 P0
?? 安全嗎,順序
---
Available＝3；Need 分別是 4、2、3。先 P1（2 ≤ 3），完成後 Available 5；再 P2（3 ≤ 5），之後 7；最後 P0（4 ≤ 7）。

@q dl3-23 | os-deadlock#3 | 3 | 計算
接上題，P0 再申請 3 個。Banker 准不准？
== 不准
---
假設給了：P0 已配 6、Need 1，Available 0。三個行程的 Need 都 > 0，沒有人能保證完成，是不安全狀態，所以拒絕（P0 先等）。資源其實夠，但給了可能讓大家卡住。

@q pr2-23 | os-process#2 | 2 | 追蹤
```
for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
    if (fork() == 0) break;
```
執行完後總共有幾個行程（含原本的）？
== 3
---
子行程 fork 回傳 0 就 break，不會再生。只有原本的行程迴圈兩次、生 2 個子行程。和「每個行程都繼續迴圈」的 4 個不同。

@q pr2-24 | os-process#2 | 2 | 陷阱
```
int x = 5;
if (fork() == 0) { x++; exit(0); }
wait(NULL);
printf("%d\n", x);
```
父行程印出什麼？
== 5
---
fork 之後父子各有自己的位址空間（copy-on-write 只是實作上的省事），子行程改的是自己的 x。要互相看到得用共享記憶體或其他 IPC。

@q pr2-25 | os-process#2 | 3 | 情境
shell 執行 `ls > out.txt` 時，大致怎麼做到輸出導向檔案？
[x] fork 之後在子行程裡開 out.txt，把它複製到標準輸出（檔案描述元 1）的位置，再 exec ls；exec 後開著的描述元會保留
[ ] 讓 ls 自己知道要寫檔
[ ] 父行程讀 ls 的輸出再寫檔
[ ] 修改 ls 的程式碼
---
ls 完全不知道自己被導向，它只是寫標準輸出。這就是「fork 和 exec 分開」的好處：中間可以調整子行程的環境。

@q pr5-21 | os-process#5 | 2 | 計算
訊息傳遞要把資料從傳送者複製到核心、再從核心複製到接收者。複製速度 10 GB/s，傳 1 GB 的資料光複製要多少秒？用共享記憶體呢？
== 0.2,0
?? 訊息傳遞,共享記憶體
---
兩次複製共 2 GB，0.2 秒。共享記憶體不用複製，只要同步存取。大量資料常用共享記憶體，小訊息用訊息傳遞比較簡單安全。

@q pr5-22 | os-process#5 | 3 | 情境
父行程建 pipe、fork 出子行程跑 `wc`（讀 pipe），父行程寫完資料後關掉自己的寫入端，但 wc 一直不結束。最可能的原因？
[x] 子行程自己也繼承了寫入端卻沒關；只要還有任何寫入端開著，讀的一方就讀不到 EOF，會一直等
[ ] pipe 緩衝區太小
[ ] wc 壞了
[ ] 父行程寫太快
---
fork 後每一方都要關掉自己用不到的那一端，這是 pipe 程式最常見的 bug。

@q pr5-23 | os-process#5 | 2 | 比較
Compare signals (such as `SIGINT`) with pipes as ways for processes to communicate. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] A signal carries only a small notification, essentially the signal number, and arrives asynchronously.
[x] A pipe carries a stream of data of arbitrary length.
[ ] A signal can carry an arbitrary amount of data.
[x] `SIGKILL` cannot be caught or ignored by the receiving process.
[ ] Writing to a pipe interrupts the reader immediately, like a signal.
---
「訊號只是通知、非同步」對：收到時會打斷行程目前在做的事，去執行處理函式。

「pipe 傳任意長度的資料」對。

「訊號能帶任意資料」錯：基本上只有一個編號。

「`SIGKILL` 不能被攔或忽略」對：保證系統一定能終止行程。

「寫 pipe 會立刻打斷讀的一方」錯：讀的一方要自己去讀；沒資料時才在 `read` 那裡等。

@q vm2-21 | os-vm#2 | 3 | 陷阱
3 個 frame，參考串 7 1 2 7 3 1 4 2 7 1。FIFO、LRU、OPT 各幾次 page fault？
== 7,9,6
?? FIFO,LRU,OPT
---
這串剛好讓 LRU 每次都踢掉「下一個馬上要用」的頁，比 FIFO 還差。LRU 通常比 FIFO 好，但不保證每串都好；OPT 才是下界。

@q vm2-22 | os-vm#2 | 2 | 追蹤
Clock（second chance），3 個 frame，新載入的頁參考位元設 1。參考串 1 2 3 2 4 1 5 2，共幾次 page fault？
== 7
---
1、2、3 缺頁；2 命中。4：指針轉一圈把三個位元都清成 0，再換掉 1。之後 1、5、2 各缺頁一次，總共 7 次，只有一次命中。

@q vm2-23 | os-vm#2 | 2 | 計算
同一串 7 1 2 7 3 1 4 2 7 1，改成 4 個 frame 用 LRU，幾次 page fault？
== 7
---
多一個 frame 後，只有第一次碰到的 5 個頁（7、1、2、3、4）加上被擠掉的 2、7 再回來，共 7 次。LRU 是堆疊型演算法，frame 增加不會變差。

@q vm2-24 | os-vm#2 | 3 | 觀念
Which of the following statements about when the clock algorithm degenerates into FIFO are correct?
[x] It happens when every page's reference bit is 1.
[x] The hand then sweeps once and replaces the page where it started.
[ ] It happens whenever there are many frames, since the hand then moves slowly.
[ ] It never happens, since the reference bits always differ between pages.
[ ] It then behaves exactly like LRU, since every page was used recently.
---
參考位元「全為 1」時沒有資訊可以區分：指針轉一圈把大家清成 0，「最後換掉起點那頁」，也就是照順序換，就是 FIFO。frame 多少不是條件；所有頁都被用過時位元可能全是 1；退化成的是 FIFO，不是 LRU。

@q me3-22 | os-memory#3 | 2 | 計算
頁大小 4 KB，邏輯位址 0x1A2B3。頁號和頁內位移（十進位）各是？
== 26,691
?? 頁號,位移
---
4 KB＝$2^{12}$，最低 12 位元（3 個 16 進位數字）是位移：0x2B3＝691；其餘 0x1A＝26 是頁號。

@q me3-23 | os-memory#3 | 2 | 計算
36 位元邏輯位址、頁 8 KB、每個頁表項目 4 bytes。單層頁表要多少 MB？
== 32
---
頁內位移 13 位元，頁號 23 位元，$2^{23}$ 項 $\times4$ bytes＝$2^{25}$ bytes＝32 MB。每個行程一張，太大，所以要多層頁表。

@q st1-22 | os-storage#1 | 2 | 計算
磁柱 0～199，磁頭在 60，請求依序 20、85、40、150、110、10、70。FCFS 和 SSTF 的總移動量？
== 460,230
?? FCFS,SSTF
---
FCFS：$40+65+45+110+40+100+60=460$。SSTF：70、85、110、150、40、20、10，$10+15+25+40+110+20+10=230$。

@q st1-23 | os-storage#1 | 2 | 計算
同一組請求，磁頭往大的方向移動。LOOK 和 C-LOOK（回頭跳到最小的請求、跳的距離也算）的總移動量？
== 230,260
?? LOOK,C-LOOK
---
LOOK：60→150（90）再回頭到 10（140），共 230。C-LOOK：60→150（90），跳到 10（140），再往上到 40（30），共 260。C-LOOK 的等待時間比較平均，總移動量不一定較少。

@q st1-24 | os-storage#1 | 1 | 計算
15000 RPM 的硬碟，平均旋轉延遲幾 ms？
== 2
---
一圈 $60/15000=4$ ms，平均要等半圈。

@q st1-25 | os-storage#1 | 2 | 計算
平均尋軌 4 ms、平均旋轉延遲 2 ms，讀 4 KB（傳輸 200 MB/s）。每秒大約能做幾次隨機讀取？
== 166
== 166.1
---
每次約 $4+2+0.02=6.02$ ms，$1000/6.02\approx166$。傳輸時間幾乎可以忽略，隨機存取完全被機械動作拖住。

@q sy2-22 | os-sync#2 | 2 | 找錯
A student writes the entry code for process $P_i$ (with $j = 1 - i$) as
`while (flag[j]) ;  flag[i] = true;`
that is, wait until the other flag is down and then raise one's own flag. Both flags start false. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Mutual exclusion can be violated.
[x] One bad order is: $P_0$ sees `flag[1]` false, $P_1$ sees `flag[0]` false, then both set their flags and enter.
[ ] Waiting before raising one's own flag guarantees mutual exclusion.
[ ] The original order (raise one's own flag first, then wait) can also let both processes into their critical sections at once.
[ ] On a single-core machine this code is safe, because only one process runs at a time.
---
「互斥可能被破壞」對、「交錯的例子」對：檢查和舉旗之間有空隙，兩人都在對方舉旗之前檢查完。

「先等再舉旗保證互斥」錯：如上。

「原本的順序也會一起進」錯：先舉旗就不會兩人同時進去；它的問題是兩人都舉了旗就一起卡在 while。Peterson 用 `turn` 解決這個問題。

「單核就安全」錯：在檢查完、還沒舉旗時被切換，就會發生上面的交錯。

@q sy5-21 | os-sync#5 | 2 | 情境
監視器中有 10 條執行緒在同一個條件上等，但資源一次只夠一個人用。持有者放開時呼叫 `broadcast()`。最後有幾條執行緒會在重新檢查條件後又回去睡？
== 9
---
10 條全醒，輪流重新取得監視器、用 while 重檢條件，只有 1 條成功，其他 9 條又回去睡（大家一起醒的「驚群」）。這種情況用 signal 叫一個就好。

@q sy5-22 | os-sync#5 | 3 | 陷阱
A monitor is implemented with one ordinary (non-recursive) pthread mutex: every monitor procedure locks it on entry and unlocks it on exit. The procedure `put()` calls the procedure `count()` of the same monitor while it still holds the mutex. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The thread waits forever, because `count()` tries to lock a mutex that the same thread already holds.
[x] One fix is to have both procedures call an internal helper that does not lock.
[ ] Since the same thread holds the mutex, `count()` enters automatically.
[ ] A recursive mutex would hang in exactly the same way, since it is still a mutex.
[ ] The problem can happen only on a multi-core machine.
---
「同一條執行緒自己卡住」對：一般的 mutex 不記得是誰拿的，第二次上鎖就等「自己」放鎖，永遠等不到。

「呼叫不上鎖的內部函式」對：把 `count()` 的主體抽成內部函式，公開的程序負責上鎖，內部互相呼叫時用不上鎖的版本。

「同一條執行緒會自動進去」錯：非遞迴的 mutex 不會這樣做。

「遞迴 mutex 一樣卡住」錯：遞迴 mutex 記錄擁有者和次數，擁有者可以再上鎖，解鎖同樣次數後才真的放掉，這也是一種解法。

「只在多核心發生」錯：跟核心數無關，單核也一樣卡住。

@q sy5-23 | os-sync#5 | 2 | 比較
Compare the `wait` and `signal` operations on a condition variable inside a monitor with the `wait` (P) and `signal` (V) operations on a semaphore. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Calling `wait` on a condition variable always blocks the caller until another process signals that condition variable.
[x] Calling `wait` on a semaphore whose value is positive returns immediately after decrementing the value.
[ ] If `signal` is called on a condition variable while no process is waiting on it, the signal is remembered, so the next `wait` on it returns immediately.
[x] If `signal` is called on a semaphore while no process is waiting, the value is incremented, so a later `wait` can pass without waiting.
[ ] Calling `wait` on a condition variable decrements a counter stored in the condition variable.
---
「條件變數的 wait 一定會睡」對：它沒有計數，只是一個等待佇列。所以呼叫前要先檢查條件，條件不成立才 wait。

「號誌值為正時 wait 直接通過」對：減 1 就回來，不會睡。

「沒人等時條件變數的 signal 會被記住」錯：沒人等的 signal 直接消失，之後的 wait 照樣睡。這是用條件變數時最常見的漏訊號 bug。

「號誌沒人等時 signal 會加 1」對：號誌的值就是「記住的 signal 次數」，之後的 wait 可以直接用掉。

「條件變數的 wait 會減計數」錯：條件變數沒有值，減 1 是號誌的行為。

@q sy6-21 | os-sync#6 | 3 | 觀念
Three tasks have priorities $T_{hi} > T_{mid} > T_{lo}$. $T_{hi}$ waits for a lock held by $T_{mid}$, and $T_{mid}$ waits for another lock held by $T_{lo}$. Priority inheritance is used. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] $T_{lo}$ temporarily runs at the priority of $T_{hi}$.
[x] The inheritance must pass along the whole chain of waiting tasks.
[ ] $T_{lo}$ is raised only to the priority of $T_{mid}$.
[x] Without inheritance, some other medium-priority task could keep preempting $T_{lo}$ and delay $T_{hi}$ for a long time.
[ ] Priority inheritance lowers the priority of $T_{hi}$ while it waits.
---
「$T_{lo}$ 暫時升到 $T_{hi}$ 的優先權」對、「沿等待鏈傳遞」對：$T_{hi}$ 實際上在等 $T_{lo}$ 放鎖（$T_{mid}$ 才能繼續、才能放鎖），所以 $T_{lo}$ 要用 $T_{hi}$ 的優先權跑。

「只升到 $T_{mid}$」錯：那樣其他中高優先權的工作還是能搶走 $T_{lo}$，$T_{hi}$ 照樣被拖住。

「沒有繼承時會被其他中優先權工作拖住」對：這就是優先權反轉。

「降低 $T_{hi}$」錯：繼承是把持鎖者往上提，不是把等待者往下壓。

@q sy6-22 | os-sync#6 | 2 | 情境
On a Mars rover, a low-priority weather task holds the bus lock, a high-priority bus-management task waits for that lock, and a medium-priority communications task keeps running. A watchdog sees that the high-priority task has not finished in time and keeps resetting the system. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Enabling priority inheritance on that lock fixes the problem.
[x] This is a case of priority inversion.
[ ] Adding more memory fixes the problem.
[x] The medium-priority task keeps the weather task from running, so the weather task cannot release the lock.
[ ] Turning off the watchdog is a proper fix.
---
「開優先權繼承」對：氣象工作持鎖時暫時升到高優先權，中優先權的工作就搶不走它。這是 1997 年 Mars Pathfinder 的真實事件，工程師從地球遠端改了設定就修好了。

「優先權反轉」對。

「加記憶體」錯：跟記憶體無關。

「中優先權讓氣象工作跑不到」對：這是高優先權一直等的原因。

「關掉 watchdog」錯：重開機停了，但高優先權工作還是會延誤。

@q dl4-21 | os-deadlock#4 | 3 | 比較
A database uses timestamps to keep transactions from waiting for each other in a cycle. Which of the following statements about the wound-wait rule are correct?
[x] When an older transaction needs a resource held by a younger one, the younger one is rolled back.
[x] When a younger transaction needs a resource held by an older one, it waits.
[x] A rolled-back transaction keeps its original timestamp, so it is not sacrificed forever.
[ ] When a younger transaction needs a resource held by an older one, the younger one wounds the older one.
[ ] A rolled-back transaction receives a new, later timestamp each time it restarts.
---
wound-wait：「老的要年輕的資源，年輕的回滾」；「年輕的要老的資源就等」。等待只有「年輕等老」一個方向，不會成環。回滾的交易「保留原本的時間戳記」，越來越老，終究不會再被犧牲；如果每次拿新的時間戳記，就可能一直被犧牲。另一種 wait-die 剛好相反。

@q st4-21 | os-storage#4 | 3 | 計算
Unix 中，一個目錄底下有 3 個子目錄（以及一些一般檔案）。這個目錄的 hard link 數是多少？
== 5
---
父目錄裡指向它的名字 1 個、它自己的「.」1 個、每個子目錄的「..」各 1 個：$1+1+3=5$。一般檔案不影響。

@q st5-21 | os-storage#5 | 2 | 計算
每筆交易寫 3 個 metadata 區塊加 1 個 commit 區塊，每塊 4 KB。每秒 1000 筆交易，日誌每秒要寫多少 MB？
== 16
---
$4\times4$ KB＝16 KB／筆，乘 1000＝16 MB/s（以 1000 KB＝1 MB 估）。因為日誌是循序寫入，比隨機改原位置快得多，所以這個成本可以接受。

@q st5-22 | os-storage#5 | 3 | 情境
硬碟有自己的寫入快取，可能把收到的寫入重新排序。日誌式檔案系統要怎麼確保「日誌內容先落地、commit 記錄後落地」？
[x] 在寫 commit 之前送出「把快取內容寫到碟片」的命令（或用強制直寫的寫入），確保前面的資料真的落地
[ ] 什麼都不用做
[ ] 關掉日誌
[ ] 把 commit 寫兩次
---
如果順序被打亂，當機後可能看到 commit 但日誌內容不完整，重放就會寫入垃圾。

@q me5-21 | os-memory#5 | 3 | 情境
x86 硬體同時支援分段和分頁。現代 64 位元作業系統通常怎麼用分段？
[x] 幾乎不用：程式和資料段的 base 設 0、limit 設到最大（平坦模型），真正的轉換和保護都交給分頁
[ ] 只用分段、不用分頁
[ ] 每個函式一個段
[ ] 每個行程用不同的段號
---
分頁沒有外部碎片、配置簡單、可攜性好，所以成為主流；分段只留下少數特殊用途（例如執行緒區域變數的基底）。

@q me5-22 | os-memory#5 | 1 | 計算
A pure contiguous-segmentation allocator has 3 separate free holes. Their sizes are 100, 50 and 150 KiB. Every pair of these holes is separated by live storage or by memory outside the allocator's pool, and the table lists all unused memory available for this request. Existing live allocations cannot move, and no operation is allowed to treat their contents as unused.

1 new request needs a single segment of 200 contiguous KiB. It is not 3 independent requests whose contents could be scattered. The segment model has 1 physical base and 1 length, so it cannot silently become a paged mapping spread over several separated frames. Metadata and alignment costs are excluded, making the stated hole sizes exact. A diagnostic reports the total free total, while the placement algorithm also checks the largest individual region. Determine what each measurement establishes and whether the request can be honored under the stated rules. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Total unused memory is 300 KiB.
[x] The largest existing hole is 150 KiB.
[x] The total unused memory alone does not guarantee success.
[x] The request fails under the stated contiguous-allocation restriction.
[x] Under these rules, the allocator may not split this one segment between separated holes.
---

@q sc3-23 | os-sched#3 | 3 | 陷阱
A real-time system usually runs 6 periodic tasks with total utilization 0.8, but during a sensor burst the utilization briefly rises to 1.2, so not every deadline can be met. The engineers compare how earliest-deadline-first (EDF) and rate-monotonic (RM) scheduling behave in such an overload: which tasks miss their deadlines, whether the misses stay limited to a few tasks, and whether the two algorithms behave the same way because both are optimal in their own class. The brake-control task has the shortest period, 2 ms. Which of the following statements about EDF and RM under overload ($U>1$) are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] Under EDF, many tasks may miss their deadlines together in a cascade.
[ ] Under overload, neither EDF nor RM misses any deadline.
[ ] Under RM, the missed deadlines are spread evenly over all of the tasks.
[ ] EDF and RM behave the same under overload, since both are optimal.
[ ] Under EDF, only the task with the longest period can miss its deadline.
---
EDF 誰快到期就跑誰，超載時大家一起拖，「可能連鎖錯過」。$U>1$ 時一定有工作錯過；RM 固定優先權，錯過的集中在低優先權工作，不是平均分攤，這也是安全關鍵系統有時寧用 RM 的原因；EDF 錯過的不一定只有週期最長的那個。

@q sy1-23 | os-sync#1 | 3 | 計算
兩條執行緒各對共享變數 count（初值 0）做 1000 次 `count++`（讀、加、寫三步），完全沒有同步。最後 count 的最小可能值？
== 2
---
經典的極端交錯：T1 讀到 0 後停住；T2 做 999 次；T1 寫回 1；T2 讀到 1 後停住；T1 做完剩下 999 次；T2 寫回 2。結果遠小於直覺的 1000。

@q vm3-22 | os-vm#3 | 2 | 計算

== 3,2
?? 第6個,第9個
---
第 4～6 個參考是 3 2 4，大小 3；第 7～9 個是 4 4 3，大小 2。程式進入只用少數頁的階段時，工作集會縮小。

@q vm4-22 | os-vm#4 | 3 | 計算

@q vm4-23 | os-vm#4 | 2 | 情境

[x] `mmap` saves one copy from the kernel's page cache into a user buffer.
[x] With `mmap`, the first touch of each page still causes a page fault.
[x] The data must still be read from disk into the page cache.
[x] The file must still be opened to obtain a descriptor for `mmap`.
[ ] `mmap` avoids page faults entirely, since the whole file is mapped at once.
---

@q me4-23 | os-memory#4 | 2 | 計算

@q pr1-23 | os-process#1 | 2 | 陷阱
On a machine, a process uses 400 MB of memory, and the disk can write about 200 MB per second. The kernel performs a context switch from this process to another one. Saving the outgoing process's 32 registers and program counter and loading those of the incoming process takes on the order of a microsecond. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The kernel saves the outgoing process's registers and program counter in its PCB and loads those of the incoming process.
[x] The 400 MB of memory contents of the outgoing process stay where they are; they are not written to disk.
[ ] The entire memory of the outgoing process is written to disk at every context switch.
[x] Moving a whole process out to disk is a separate and much rarer mechanism, used when memory is short.
[x] If each switch had to write the 400 MB image to this disk, a single switch would take about 2 s.
---

@q pr1-24 | os-process#1 | 2 | 計算

@q pr3-23 | os-process#3 | 3 | 情境

== 80
---

@q sc2-26 | os-sched#2 | 2 | 計算

@q sy3-23 | os-sync#3 | 2 | 情境
Kernel code on a single-core machine protects a very short section with a spinlock. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] If the holder of the spinlock is switched out, another thread that spins on the lock wastes its whole time slice, because the holder cannot run to release the lock.
[x] Preventing the scheduler from switching away while the lock is held makes the holder finish the short section quickly.
[ ] Spinlocks are the best choice on a single core for long critical sections.
[x] If an interrupt handler may take the same lock, interrupts must be disabled while the lock is held; otherwise the handler may spin forever.
[ ] Disabling interrupts is a good way for ordinary user programs to protect their critical sections.
---
「持有者被換下，等的人整個時間片白轉」對：單核時持有者不跑就不可能放鎖。

「持有期間不讓排程器換人」對：Linux 的 spinlock 會關搶占，確保很快做完放鎖。

「長臨界區用自旋最好」錯：等的人一直空轉浪費 CPU，長的要用會睡的鎖。

「中斷處理也要同一把鎖時要關中斷」對：否則持有者被中斷打斷，處理程式去拿鎖，永遠等不到被它打斷的持有者，卡死。

「使用者程式關中斷」錯：關中斷是特權指令；而且多核時只關本核的中斷也擋不住別的核心。

@q me3-24 | os-memory#3 | 2 | 計算
邏輯位址空間有 128 頁、每頁 2 KB；實體記憶體有 64 個 frame。邏輯位址和實體位址各幾位元？
== 18,17
?? 邏輯,實體
---
頁內位移 11 位元（2 KB）。邏輯：頁號 7 位元＋11＝18。實體：frame 號 6 位元＋11＝17。

@q vm2-25 | os-vm#2 | 3 | 陷阱
FIFO 頁替換，參考串 1 3 2 5 2 1 3 4 1 3 2 5 4。3 個 frame 和 4 個 frame 各幾次 page fault？
== 9,10
?? 3個frame,4個frame
---
frame 變多 fault 反而變多，這就是 Belady 異常；LRU、OPT 這類堆疊型演算法不會發生。異常不只出現在課本那一串，只是很少見。

@q st3-24 | os-storage#3 | 2 | 計算
inode 有 12 個直接指標、1 個單間接、1 個雙間接；區塊 1 KB、指標 4 bytes。最大檔案約多少 KB？
== 65804
---
每個間接區塊 256 個指標：$12+256+256^2=65804$ 個區塊，每塊 1 KB。區塊變成 4 KB 時，間接區塊也變大（1024 個指標），最大檔案大很多。

@q dl3-24 | os-deadlock#3 | 3 | 計算
Banker：Available＝(1,1,2)。Allocation：P0 (1,0,1)、P1 (2,1,0)、P2 (0,1,1)、P3 (1,0,0)；Need：P0 (1,2,1)、P1 (0,1,2)、P2 (1,0,1)、P3 (0,1,2)。系統安全嗎？一開始就能被滿足的行程有幾個？
== 安全,2
?? 安全嗎,一開始可滿足
---
一開始 P1 (0,1,2) 和 P2 (1,0,1) 都 ≤ (1,1,2)。例如 P1 完成後 Available (3,2,2)，再 P2 (3,3,3)、P3 (4,3,3)、最後 P0。


@q me2-23 | os-memory#2 | 2 | 追蹤
空洞依序 300、200、500、100（KB），依序請求 150、180、120。first-fit 和 next-fit 中，120 各放在哪個空洞（第幾個）？
== 1,3
?? first-fit,next-fit
---
first-fit：150→第 1 個（剩 150）、180→第 2 個、120→第 1 個（剩 30）。next-fit 從上次的位置往後找：150→第 1 個、180→第 2 個、120 從第 2 個往後找到第 3 個（500）。next-fit 讓大空洞更快被切碎。


@q dl4-23 | os-deadlock#4 | 3 | 計算
等待關係有三個環：{P1,P2}、{P2,P3}、{P3,P4}。終止成本 P1 3、P2 5、P3 3、P4 4。要打斷所有環的最小總成本？
== 6
---
選 P1 和 P3：P1 打斷第一個環，P3 同時打斷後兩個，$3+3=6$。只殺共同的 P2 打不斷 {P3,P4}；P2＋P3＝8、P2＋P4＝9 都比較貴。


@q me4-24 | os-memory#4 | 2 | 計算
32 位元、頁 4 KB、10/10/12 兩層頁表，每張表 4 KB。行程只用位址 0 開始連續的 64 MB。頁表共佔多少 KB？
== 68
---
每張內層表涵蓋 4 MB，64 MB 要 16 張，加外層 1 張，$17\times4$ KB。


@q me1-23 | os-memory#1 | 2 | 觀念
A small embedded program keeps 20 KiB of permanent code in memory at all times. It also has two routines that are never needed at the same time: a 50 KiB routine for setup and a 70 KiB routine for normal operation. To save memory, the programmer reserves one overlay area that holds either routine, and a small piece of the permanent code loads one routine over the other when the program switches phases. The device has exactly 96 KiB of memory for code, and loading buffers can be ignored. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The overlay area can hold either routine if it provides 70 KiB.
[x] Permanent code plus the overlay need at least 90 KiB in this model.
[ ] Both routines must remain resident simultaneously for overlays to work.
[x] The routines may reuse the same physical overlay area at different times.
[x] A total of 70 KiB is not enough for the permanent code plus the larger routine.
---
「70 KiB 覆蓋區」對：只要容納較大的那個。「總共 90 KiB」對：20＋70＝90。「必同時在」錯：題設恰好互斥使用。「不同時刻共用區域」對：用新需要的程式覆蓋舊的。「總共 70 不夠」對：還有必須一直保留的 20 KiB。


@q dl2-24 | os-deadlock#2 | 2 | 追蹤
每種資源各一個。P1 持有 R1、等 R2；P2 持有 R2、等 R3；P3 持有 R3、等 R4；P4 持有 R4、不再申請。有死結嗎？行程完成的順序？
== 沒有,P4 P3 P2 P1
?? 死結,順序
---
等待圖 P1→P2→P3→P4 是一條鏈、沒有環。P4 完成放出 R4，P3 拿到後完成，依序往回。


@q sc4-24 | os-sched#4 | 2 | 計算
行程被遷移到另一顆核心後，要把約 2 MB 的工作資料重新載入那顆核心的 cache，記憶體頻寬 20 GB/s。大約多花多少 ms？
== 0.1
---
$2\text{ MB}/20\text{ GB/s}\approx0.1$ ms。時間片只有幾 ms 時，這個成本不小，所以排程器偏好讓行程留在原來的核心（親和性）。



@q sc1-24 | os-sched#1 | 1 | 計算
行程在時間 2 到達，時間 5 第一次拿到 CPU，一口氣執行 7 個單位，在時間 12 完成。等待時間和周轉時間？
== 3,10
?? 等待,周轉
---
等待 $5-2=3$（也等於回應時間，因為中途沒被換下來）；周轉 $12-2=10$＝等待＋執行。


@q sc3-24 | os-sched#3 | 1 | 計算
三個週期性工作的週期是 4、6、10。超週期（排程表開始重複的長度）是多少？
== 60
---
最小公倍數 $\text{lcm}(4,6,10)=60$。模擬排程或檢查期限只要看一個超週期。


@q st2-23 | os-storage#2 | 2 | 計算
4 顆硬碟做 RAID 0，每顆一年內故障的比例是 2%（彼此獨立）。整個陣列一年內資料遺失的可能性大約多少（%）？
== 7.8
== 7.76
---
任何一顆壞掉就全毀：$1-0.98^4\approx7.8\%$。速度變 4 倍，可靠度變差將近 4 倍。


@q st4-23 | os-storage#4 | 1 | 計算
開啟 /usr/local/bin/gcc（目前什麼都沒有快取）。要依序讀幾個目錄的內容來找到 gcc 的 inode？
== 4
---
根目錄、usr、local、bin 各讀一次。所以系統會快取目錄項目（dentry cache），常用路徑不必每次重讀。


@q sy1-25 | os-sync#1 | 2 | 情境
Two threads run `if (instance == NULL) instance = create();` at the same time, where `instance` is shared and initially `NULL`. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Both threads may see `NULL` and both call `create()`, so two objects are created and one of them is lost.
[x] Putting the check and the creation inside the same lock, or using a run-once facility such as `pthread_once`, fixes the problem.
[ ] This cannot happen, because an assignment to a pointer is a single instruction.
[x] This is a check-then-act race condition.
[ ] Repeating the `NULL` check twice without any lock fixes the problem.
---
「兩條都看到 `NULL`、各建一個」對：檢查和建立之間有空隙。

「放在同一把鎖裡或用只執行一次的機制」對。

「指定是一條指令所以不會」錯：問題在「檢查」和「指定」是兩個步驟，不是指定本身。

「check-then-act 競爭條件」對。

「不加鎖、檢查兩次」錯：兩次檢查之間一樣有空隙；所謂 double-checked locking 必須在第二次檢查時拿鎖，還要注意記憶體順序。


@q sy4-23 | os-sync#4 | 1 | 計算
有限緩衝區 10 格，empty 初值 10、full 初值 0。生產者放了 15 個、消費者拿了 8 個（都已完成）。empty 和 full 現在是？
== 3,7
?? empty,full
---
緩衝區裡 $15-8=7$ 個，full＝7、empty＝3，兩者和永遠是 10（沒有人卡在中間時）。


@q vm3-23 | os-vm#3 | 2 | 情境
三個行程的工作集大小 10、20、30 個 frame，系統只有 50 個 frame。要暫停一個行程避免 thrashing，最少暫停哪一個就夠？
== 10
---
總需求 60 > 50。暫停工作集 10 的那個就剩 50，剛好夠。暫停大的也行，但會讓更多工作停下。


@q me5-23 | os-memory#5 | 1 | 計算

== 200,300
?? 共用,不共用
---
共用：$100+50+50$；不共用：$2\times150$。兩個段表的程式碼段指向同一個 base 和 limit。


@q pr2-26 | os-process#2 | 2 | 追蹤
`if (fork()) fork();` 執行完後總共有幾個行程（含原本的）？
== 3
---
第一個 fork：父行程拿到正的 PID 進 if，再 fork 一次；子行程拿到 0，不進 if。所以是父、第一個子、第二個子，共 3 個。


@q pr4-24 | os-process#4 | 1 | 計算
中斷處理程式的位址表有 256 項，每項存一個 8 bytes 的處理程式位址。表佔多少 bytes？
== 2048
---
$256\times8$。硬體用中斷編號乘上每項大小，直接找到處理程式的位址。


@q sc2-27 | os-sched#2 | 2 | 計算
搶先式 SJF（SRTF）：P1（到達 0、執行 6）、P2（2、2）、P3（3、8）、P4（4、3）。平均等待時間？
== 3.25
---
P1 0–2（剩 4）；P2 到達更短，2–4；P4 到達時剩餘 3 < P1 的 4，4–7；P1 7–11；P3 11–19。等待：P1 5、P2 0、P3 8、P4 0，平均 $13/4$。


@q sc4-25 | os-sched#4 | 3 | 計算
CFS 目標延遲 20 ms、最小時間片 4 ms。有 100 個可執行、權重相同的行程時，每人分到多少 ms？輪完一圈要多久？
== 4,400
?? 每人,一圈
---
$20/100=0.2$ ms 太小，切換開銷會吃掉 CPU，所以用最小時間片 4 ms，一圈變成 $100\times4=400$ ms。行程很多時，回應時間就會變長。


@q sy3-24 | os-sync#3 | 1 | 追蹤
lock 初值 0。三條執行緒依序各呼叫一次 `test_and_set(&lock)`（沒有人放鎖）。三次的回傳值？
== 0,1,1
---
第一次讀到 0（拿到鎖）並設成 1；之後讀到的都是 1（沒拿到）。回傳舊值是判斷有沒有拿到鎖的關鍵。


@q dl3-25 | os-deadlock#3 | 1 | 計算
Banker：某行程的 Max＝(4,3,2)、Allocation＝(1,2,0)。它的 Need？
== 3,1,2
---
Need ＝ Max − Allocation，代表它最多還會再要多少。安全演算法拿 Need 和 Available 比。


@q me3-25 | os-memory#3 | 1 | 計算
實體記憶體 4 GB、頁 4 KB。頁表項目中的 frame 編號至少要幾位元？
== 20
---
frame 數 $2^{32}/2^{12}=2^{20}$。項目還要放 valid、dirty、參考、保護等位元，通常湊成 32 位元。


@q vm4-24 | os-vm#4 | 1 | 計算
2 MB 的大頁，頁內位移有幾位元？
== 21
---
$2\text{ MB}=2^{21}$ bytes。一般 4 KB 頁是 12 位元；大頁讓 TLB 每一項涵蓋 512 倍的範圍。

@q me3-26 | os-memory#3 | 3 | 計算
TLB 有 64 項（滿了就換掉最久沒用的）、頁 4 KB。程式從頭到尾循序掃過一個 1 MB 的陣列，連續掃兩遍。總共幾次 TLB miss？
== 512
---
1 MB 是 256 頁。第一遍每頁第一次碰到都 miss；第二遍開始時 TLB 只留著最後 64 頁，而掃描又從第 0 頁開始，LRU 每次踢掉的正好是之後要用的，又是 256 次。陣列只要再大一點點超過 TLB 涵蓋範圍，就會這樣全部 miss。


@q pr5-24 | os-process#5 | 1 | 計算
pipe 的緩衝區 64 KB。寫的一方每次寫 4 KB，讀的一方暫時沒在讀。寫的一方最多能先寫幾次才會被擋住？
== 16
---
$64/4=16$。緩衝區讓兩邊的速度可以暫時不一樣；滿了寫的一方就睡，空了讀的一方就睡。


@q vm1-23 | os-vm#1 | 1 | 計算
程式依序讀過 2000 個不同的頁，每頁只用一次，系統給它 500 個 frame（需求分頁）。共幾次 page fault？
== 2000
---
每頁都是第一次用，一定缺頁；而且不會再用，替換誰都一樣。這種串流式存取，frame 再多也沒有幫助（預先載入才有用）。


@q st1-26 | os-storage#1 | 1 | 計算

== 2.5
---
$250/100$。放大越大，SSD 越快磨損；預留空間、TRIM 都能降低它。


@q sc3-25 | os-sched#3 | 2 | 計算
On one processor, independent preemptible periodic tasks have (period,execution) pairs (5,2), (7,3), (10,2). All first jobs arrive at time 0, deadlines equal periods, execution always needs the stated amount, and scheduling overhead is zero. The workload continues indefinitely. Compare the total required processor fraction with one processor's available fraction before applying EDF optimality or the RM sufficient bound. A priority rule cannot create additional execution time. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The total utilization is about 1.03, which exceeds 1.
[x] No scheduling algorithm can meet all deadlines for this task set.
[ ] EDF meets all deadlines here, because EDF is optimal.
[ ] All deadlines are met, because each task's own utilization is below 1.
[ ] RM meets all deadlines here, because the shortest period gets the highest priority.
---
$\frac25+\frac37+\frac2{10}\approx1.03$，「超過 1」，長期下來 CPU 根本做不完，「任何演算法都不行」。EDF 的最佳是在 $U\le1$ 的前提下；看的是總使用率，不是個別的；RM 更不可能。



@q dl4-24 | os-deadlock#4 | 2 | 情境

[ ] 印到一半的印表機
[ ] 燒錄到一半的光碟機
[ ] 正在寫入的資料庫交易鎖（不能回滾的系統）
---
可以存下狀態、之後還原的資源才適合搶占；印到一半的工作被搶走只能重印。

== 98
---
$(64-50)+(128-100)+(256-200)=14+28+56$。固定分割沒有外部碎片，但分割區內用不到的部分都浪費了。


@q st5-23 | os-storage#5 | 2 | 計算
日誌區是 128 MB 的環狀空間，日誌以 16 MB/s 寫入。如果都不做 checkpoint（把已 commit 的修改寫回原位、釋放日誌空間），幾秒後日誌就滿了？

---

@q sy6-23 | os-sync#6 | 2 | 情境
A popular lock is often held by one slow thread, and many fast threads pile up waiting behind it, so overall throughput drops sharply (a lock convoy). Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Every thread that needs the lock is slowed down to the pace of the slow holder.
[x] Shortening the critical section or splitting one big lock into several finer locks reduces the problem.
[ ] Adding more threads makes the program faster in this situation.
[x] Giving each thread its own data, or using lock-free structures, reduces contention on the lock.
[ ] A lock convoy is a deadlock: the threads can never make progress.
---
「大家被拖到持有者的速度」對：鎖一次只給一個人，排在後面的快執行緒只能等。

「縮短臨界區、拆成細鎖」對：持有時間變短、不同資料用不同的鎖，排隊的人就少。

「加更多執行緒會更快」錯：只會讓隊伍更長。

「各自的資料或無鎖結構」對：從根本上減少搶同一把鎖。

「是死結」錯：仍在前進，只是很慢。



@q me5-24 | os-memory#5 | 2 | 計算
段表放在記憶體，記錄段表起點的暫存器 STBR＝0x1000，每項 8 bytes。查第 3 段時，硬體讀哪個位址的段表項？
== 1018
== 0x1018
---
$0x1000+3\times8=0x1018$。段表在記憶體裡，每次存取多一次記憶體讀取，所以也需要類似 TLB 的快取。



@q sy2-24 | os-sync#2 | 2 | 陷阱
In Peterson's solution for $P_0$ and $P_1$, does the initial value of the shared variable `turn` matter? Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The initial value of `turn` does not affect correctness.
[x] Each process sets `turn` before testing it, so the initial value is overwritten before it is ever used.
[ ] `turn` must start at 0.
[x] Both flags must start false; if `flag[1]` started true and $P_1$ never ran, $P_0$ could wait forever.
[ ] `turn` must start equal to the number of the process that runs first.
---
「`turn` 初值不影響」對、「進入前一定先寫 `turn`」對：`while` 檢查時讀到的是某個行程剛寫進去的值。

「一定要從 0 開始」錯。

「旗子要從 false 開始」對：$P_0$ 設 `turn = 1` 後檢查 `flag[1] && turn == 1`，若 `flag[1]` 一開始就是 true 而 $P_1$ 從不執行，$P_0$ 永遠等。

「要等於先跑的那個」錯：同第一句。


@q sy1-26 | os-sync#1 | 2 | 計算
共享變數 x 初值 0。兩條執行緒各做一次 `x = x + 2`（讀、加、寫，沒有同步）。x 最後可能是哪些值？
== 2,4
---
依序執行得 4；兩邊都先讀到 0 再各自寫回 2，結果 2。少掉的那次更新就是「遺失更新」。


@q sy4-24 | os-sync#4 | 1 | 追蹤
號誌 S 初值 0。T1：`wait(S); print("A");`；T2：`print("B"); signal(S);`。不論誰先開始跑，輸出一定是？
== BA
---
T1 在 S 為 0 時會被擋住，直到 T2 印完 B、signal。初值 0 的號誌就是用來表達「先後順序」。


@q sy5-24 | os-sync#5 | 3 | 情境
A bounded buffer is written as a monitor, and producers and consumers share a single condition variable, using `signal` (which wakes one waiter). Producers wait when the buffer is full; consumers wait when it is empty. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Each `signal` wakes only one waiter, so a `signal` meant for consumers may wake another producer instead, leaving the consumers asleep; eventually every thread may be asleep.
[x] Using two condition variables, one for "not full" and one for "not empty", fixes the problem.
[ ] One condition variable with `signal` always works, because some thread is always woken up.
[x] Using `broadcast` instead of `signal` also fixes it, provided every waiter rechecks its condition in a `while` loop.
[ ] With `broadcast`, checking the condition once with `if` is safe.
---
「叫醒同類，大家可能全睡」對：例如消費者拿走一個、想叫醒生產者，卻叫醒另一個在等的消費者；它發現還是空的又睡了，生產者沒人叫。

「兩個條件變數」對：各叫各的，不會叫錯人。

「總會叫醒某個人所以沒事」錯：叫錯人等於沒叫。

「改用 `broadcast` 也行，但要用 `while`」對：全部叫醒，各自重新檢查，不符合的再睡回去。

「`broadcast` 加 `if` 就安全」錯：被叫醒的好幾個人中可能只有一個能做，其他人用 `if` 不重新檢查就會出錯。


@q sy6-24 | os-sync#6 | 1 | 計算
用 aging 避免飢餓：優先權數字 127 最低、0 最高，等待的行程每 10 秒優先權數字減 1。最低優先權的行程最多等多久就會升到最高？
== 1270
---
$127\times10$ 秒，約 21 分鐘。數值決定了「最壞要等多久」。


@q dl1-24 | os-deadlock#1 | 2 | 情境
兩筆資料庫交易：T1 先鎖第 5 列再鎖第 9 列，T2 先鎖第 9 列再鎖第 5 列，偶爾互相卡死。最簡單的預防方式？
[x] 規定所有交易都照主鍵由小到大的順序上鎖，破除循環等待
[ ] 讓交易跑快一點
[ ] 把資料庫換成單執行緒
[ ] 每次只允許一筆交易
---
資料庫通常還會偵測死結並讓其中一筆回滾，但固定上鎖順序能從源頭避免。


@q me4-25 | os-memory#4 | 2 | 計算
48 位元虛擬位址、頁 4 KB、每個頁表項 8 bytes。若用單層頁表，每個行程的頁表多大（GB）？
== 512
---
$2^{36}$ 項 $\times8$ bytes $=2^{39}$ bytes＝512 GB，完全不可能。多層頁表只為實際用到的區域配置表格。


@q pr3-24 | os-process#3 | 1 | 計算
使用者層級執行緒切換約 50 ns，核心執行緒切換約 2 µs。前者快幾倍？
== 40
---
$2000/50$。使用者層級的切換不用進核心，但一條執行緒阻塞時可能拖住整個行程。


@q sc1-25 | os-sched#1 | 1 | 計算
四個工作各要 10 秒，同時到達，依序一個做完再做下一個。平均周轉時間？
== 25
---
完成時間 10、20、30、40，平均 25。吞吐量是每 10 秒一個，跟順序無關；周轉時間則會因順序而不同（長短不一時先做短的較好）。


@q st2-24 | os-storage#2 | 2 | 計算
兩顆各 200 MB/s 的硬碟做 RAID 1（鏡像）。大量循序讀和寫的頻寬最多各約多少 MB/s？
== 400,200
?? 讀,寫
---
讀可以分給兩顆同時讀不同的部分；寫要兩顆都寫同樣的資料，只有一顆的速度。


@q st5-24 | os-storage#5 | 2 | 計算
4 TB 的檔案系統，metadata 約佔 2%。當機後 fsck 要以 100 MB/s 讀過所有 metadata，大約要幾秒？
== 800
---
$4\text{ TB}\times2\%=80$ GB，$80000/100=800$ 秒。日誌式檔案系統只要重放最近的日誌，幾秒就好。



@q st4-24 | os-storage#4 | 1 | 計算
目錄用沒有排序的線性串列存 $10^4$ 個檔名。找一個存在的檔案平均要比較幾次？
== 5000
---
平均掃一半。改用雜湊表平均 $O(1)$，或用 B 樹（很多檔案系統的大目錄這樣做）$O(\lg n)$。
